# Ironlog archive

What CLAUDE.md and PENDING.md held before r54 cut them to the rules in force and the open list (V, 2026-10-18, PENDING 316). It is the code map and the history of each feature: why a rule exists, which bug or ruling made it, and where it lives. Search it for a function, setting or release when you need the reason behind something. Nothing here is a rule; CLAUDE.md is, and where the two differ CLAUDE.md wins.

## CLAUDE.md as of r53 (2026-10-18)

## Ironlog

A personal hypertrophy and strength tracker for one lifter. It is used on a phone at the gym. It ships from one source file as a standalone installable web app (dist/, hosted on GitHub Pages, offline, data on the phone). It also shipped as a Claude artifact until V retired that on 2026-09-27 and moved his log into the installed app (`OWNER.md` Done); nothing is published there any more, so never work on the artifact or republish it. The Claude-only features left the source in r46; what stays is `cloudProvider()` taking Claude's db when `window.claude` exists, which the sync suites use as their stand-in cloud. Since r24 every pull request is also published as a separate preview app at /preview/ (dist-preview/), to try on a phone before merging. Build r53, version 2.4, state schema v5. **The code is moving to TypeScript modules, one phase at a time: read `ARCHITECTURE.md` first; its rules are enforced by `tests/hygiene.js`.**

### What is in this repo

- `src/`: the source. `src/app/` holds the typed modules (TypeScript, since r49): `config.ts` is the one place for the app's name, the preview's name and label, the wordmark, the version people see (`DISPLAY_VERSION`) and the build (`APP_VERSION`), the site address and the three.js addresses. Since r50 (phase 2) `util.ts` (text, ids, dates, numbers, the sync hash), `units.ts`, `library.ts` (muscles and their bands, joints, equipment, the exercise library and what an exercise is: pattern, compound, load meaning, bodyweight share), `routines.ts` (`DEFAULT_PLAN`, the ready-made routines) and `formulas.ts` (`e1` and its inverses, `workLoad`, set counting, the time model, the thresholds) hold the code they are named for, moved unchanged (`tests/moved.js`); `types.ts` holds the record shapes. Since r51 (phase 3a) `format.ts` (dates, durations with `estMin`, counts), `layout.ts` (sections, accents, sheets with fields), `icons.ts`, `profile.ts`, `records.ts` (schema version, limits, id checks, record cleaners, `sessCmp`), `gym.ts`, `chunks.ts` and `merge.ts` (the sync documents and the three-way merge), `session.ts` (grey and effective loads, live targets, the Last column, joining a lift done twice), `loads.ts`, `prs.ts`, `trend.ts`, `volume.ts`, `goals.ts`, `charts.ts`, `importing.ts` (the import parsers) and `feedback.ts` hold theirs, and `store.ts` owns the shared state: `state`, `ui`, `cloud`, `acct` and `acctWait`, imported by the legacy script under the same names; the log is replaced only through `setState`, since an import cannot be assigned to. Since r52 (phase 3b) the engine and the analytics are functions of a model (`model.ts`: `Model` is the log, the date the engine reads as today and the caches, with `freshCaches`, `modelOf` and the accessors such as `EXIn`, `UIn`, `activeRoutineIn`, `profIn`, `gymOfIn` and `invalidateIn`): each one that reads any of it takes the model as its first parameter, `M`, and its name gains `In` (`suggestIn(M, exId, plan, opts)`). `analytics.ts` holds `IDXIn` and its six steps, `lastOf`, `statsUpToIn`, `dateIndexIn`, the RIR reading, hard sets and bodyweight; `time.ts` the time model and `paceIn`; `weeks.ts` the weekly averages, `weekStatsIn`, the streak, `recentLighterIn` and `regionBalanceIn`; `coach.ts` `coachIn`, stalls, movers and readiness; `engine.ts` `suggestIn` and its cases, steps, racks and lighter loads; and `units.ts`, `routines.ts` (`planForIn`, `defPlanIn`, `profRuleIn`), `gym.ts`, `format.ts` (the date words, `fmtDate`) and `loads.ts` (`fmtWIn`, `loadLabelIn`) grew. `live.ts` holds the one model the app runs on, `LIVE` (the store's log read at every use, util's `today`, the caches), and 74 adapters (`export const suggest = live(suggestIn);`), through which the legacy script calls them under their old names, so not one call in the screens changed. r53 is a behaviour release between phases (`ARCHITECTURE.md`): `routineUpdates` moved to `routines.ts` to change (75 adapters), `whyTarget` to `session.ts` to change its note, and `guide.ts` (the Guide, moved so its words could follow the rules it states), `providers.ts` (`AUTH`, `FB`) and `effort.ts` (`rirScale`, `halfLbl`) joined layer 4. The rest is the legacy script, being moved into `src/app/` phase by phase (`ARCHITECTURE.md`): 24 parts cut at the code's section headers in r36 (01 to 03 the page head, styles and markup; 10 to 29 the script, from start-up through storage, sync, accounts, 15 and 16 what the analytics and the load engine left behind (readers the screens show, the code that writes the log or reads the session in progress, and markup; the rest is in `src/app/` since r52), 17 adjusting the next sets, the screens, sheets, actions and events; 99 the close), pieces of one script in one function scope, kept in their original wrapper. `src/09-imports.js` holds the real `import` lines through which the legacy script reads `src/app/`. `npm run join` (`scripts/join.js`) builds `index.html`: the page parts in name order, their `{{NAME}}` placeholders filled from config (title, heading, the `app-build` meta tag), and the script bundled by esbuild into one inline script. Edit `src/`, never `index.html` by hand: `tests/r36.js` fails when the two differ.
- `index.html`: the whole app, built from `src/` and committed, because `scripts/build.js` and every test use this one file. CSS, markup, then one inline script bundled by esbuild (it keeps only some of the source's comments, so read the source in `src/`, not the page). No framework. Chart.js 4.4.1 and SortableJS 1.15.2 load from cdnjs, with jsdelivr as fallback, and the fonts come from Google Fonts. three.js 0.159 (the 3D body) is imported as a module from jsdelivr or unpkg, only when the 3D view opens; the standalone build ships its own copy in `vendor/`.
- `tests/`: Playwright suites that drive the real page in Chromium, and since 2026-10-14 the same suites in WebKit, the engine of every browser on an iPhone (Safari and the home-screen app). `tests/h.js` is the harness; `IRONLOG_BROWSER` (chromium, the default, or webkit) picks the engine, and every suite launches through its `engine`, never Playwright's `chromium` directly. It serves Chart.js, Sortable and the fonts from `node_modules`, blocks every other network call, and can fake the clock (`clock`), seed localStorage (`state`), and inject a mock cloud (`setup`).
- `scripts/build.js`: writes the standalone app to `dist/` (fonts and libraries copied in, manifest, icons, service worker); `--preview` writes the preview build to `dist-preview/`. `scripts/icons.py` draws the icons into `assets/`, and the preview's (the same mark over a striped band) into `assets/preview/`.
- `supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts`: the server function behind Delete my account (pasted into the Supabase dashboard). `supabase/functions/feedback-to-issue/index.ts`: turns each feedback report into an issue in the private tickets repository (`FEEDBACK.md`). `.github/workflows/keepalive.yml`: a daily ping so the free project never pauses. `.github/workflows/publish.yml`: `npm test` on every pull request and push to `main`, and the same suites in WebKit (`npm run test:webkit`); a push to `main` that passes both publishes `dist/` to `gh-pages`.
- `scripts/cloud-supabase.js` and `supabase.config.json`: accounts and cloud sync for the standalone app. The build copies the script into dist/ as `cloud.js` with the supabase-js bundle, and injects the project URL and publishable key (both public; row level security protects the data). The secret key never goes in this repo. `SUPABASE.md` is the dashboard setup.
- `baselines/r11.html` to `r29.html`, and `r29-5.html` (r29.5), `r30.html`, `r30-2.html` (r30.2), `r31.html`, `r31-1.html` (r31.1), `r31-2.html` (r31.2) and `r31-3.html` (r31.3), `r32.html`, `r32-1.html` (r32.1), `r34.html`, `r34-1.html` (r34.1), `r34-2.html` (r34.2), `r35.html`, `r36.html`, `r37.html`, `r38.html`, `r39.html`, `r40.html`, `r41.html`, `r42.html`, `r43.html`, `r44.html`, `r45.html`, `r46.html`, `r47.html`, `r48.html`, `r49.html`, `r50.html`, `r51.html` and `r52.html`: previous published builds (`.gitattributes` marks them, and the built `index.html`, so GitHub's language bar counts the source). `tests/migration.js` saves data with them and loads it into the current build. Never delete them. When you publish a new build, add the build it replaces here.

### Invariants (never break these; each was a ruling or a bug)

Collected in r44 from the code map below, so they are read first. The map says where each lives.

- **Data:** never lose or overwrite what the lifter typed: loads, reps, targets, settings (`refreshBlock`, `syncDraftPlan`, `rowBlank`, `appBand`). A saved set never has an empty load. What is stored is always RIR, and every set stores the number exactly as typed. A migration only adds; it never overwrites a value the user set. New optional fields stay absent when unset, so record hashes and sync are unchanged.
- **Sync across builds:** settings keys keep one order across builds (`secOrder` added last); records keep unknown fields in the order they came (`keepExtra`); a new field is relied on only once every device runs a build that keeps it ("update every device"). Never write anything in `normalize` that depends on the device (it also runs in the sync comparison, Undo and imports).
- **Numbers:** no number without a source; conventions are labelled as conventions in a tip and here under Evidence base. Trends are never shown as a 1RM; a muscle with no clear lift reads unclear, never 0%; weight lifted is never a percentage against last week; no projection anywhere (r41). Deloads and lighter loads are whole steps, never under the bar on a barbell lift typed as the total. Sessions that are deloads, lighter or trimmed never set targets or trends (`tagged`, `offSession`), but for a lift done there at its usual load and reps, which is a full session for that lift (r53, V: a day's label never overrides what a lift did; `atUsualLevel`). A PR is a PR on any day (r53).
- **V's rulings on screen:** the Volume radar is drawn from what was logged, never the routine's plan, and always draws; no Planned chip on Stats; the plan's usual-range note is a quiet line on Plan, never a flag and never on Stats; no other app is named on screen; no wording about shared phones, someone else or another person; no Comeback (former bests); no restyle without V's direction; "on target", the 1RM display and mid-workout adjustments on by default are V's calls.
- **Copy:** short, plain, specific; the number and the action on screen, the reasons in a tip or How this works; no em dashes, hype or exclamation marks; the serial comma in any list of three; estimated minutes only through `estMin` ("~N min"); the first-run Set up page stays under 400 characters, lines of 45 at most (tests/r21.js).
- **Phone:** 44 px touch targets, no sideways scroll at 320 px, light and dark both checked.
- **One source (r49, V):** a value that names, identifies or configures the app is defined once, in `src/app/config.ts`, and read everywhere else; `tests/hygiene.js` fails on the name, the site address, a build id or the storage id typed anywhere else in code or markup. Every storage key, cache name and backup mark is built from `STORAGE_ID` ("ironlog"), which never changes whatever the app is called (a phone finds its log only under those keys); `tests/r49.js` holds the keys to their exact values. `scripts/cloud-supabase.js` gets its values written in by the build (`{{NAME}}` placeholders), never from a global at run time. Since r51 the same holds for outside addresses, library versions and the font faces: config.ts defines the CDNs, the Chart.js, SortableJS and three.js versions (each equal to `package.json`'s pin), the fonts and their origins, and the password check's origin; the markup and style sheet read them through `scripts/join.js`'s placeholders, `scripts/build.js` and the account script through theirs, and any other outside address in `src/` or `scripts/` must be listed in `ARCHITECTURE.md` with the phase that moves it (`tests/hygiene.js`).
- **Modules (r49, `ARCHITECTURE.md`):** new code goes in `src/app/` as TypeScript, never in the legacy parts; the legacy script may not grow (the ratchet in `ARCHITECTURE.md`, checked by `tests/hygiene.js`); `src/app/` imports only `src/app/`; the legacy parts import only through `src/09-imports.js`; strict type checking passes. Moving code changes no code (r50): `tests/moved.js` holds each moved declaration to the build its phase started from, token for token once types and layout are set aside, with every name it uses pointing where it did and nothing else at a moved module's top level; moved code exists once (no legacy declaration takes a module export's name, every export the legacy script uses is imported, every import is used, and every name the legacy script reads without declaring or importing it is a known global; `tests/hygiene.js`). The bundle starts with "use strict", so the modules run strict as modules do (`scripts/join.js`). Since r51 the move check also holds the legacy script left behind to the baseline's, statement for statement and in order, once a phase's listed rewrites are read back (r51: `setState(x)` for `state = x`), compares with whitespace minified, and requires an executable check for anything a phase adds or changes on purpose. Each module has a layer (`ARCHITECTURE.md`): an import goes only to the same layer or below, never round in a circle, and the type checker is never switched off, in a directive (beyond the one listed `@ts-expect-error`, PENDING 304) or in `tsconfig.json` (`tests/hygiene.js`). The page is checked against the modules too: `index.html` is what `scripts/join.js` builds, and every declaration on it outside the legacy script is one module's, by name and code (`tests/moved.js`). Since r51 (the outside review V brought, `ARCHITECTURE.md`): no value has a type that is any or holds any as a union member or type argument, read with the type checker (an expression wrapped in a cast excepted, and an any deeper in an object's or function's own type beyond it); casts (`as`, type predicates, hand-typed parameters the context types any) and `!` each have a comment naming them, no cast's written type names a type parameter of a declaration around it (through aliases, interfaces and `typeof`), overloads are refused, and their counts and the open types' are a ratchet in `ARCHITECTURE.md` that only falls; every `catch` and promise handler in a module ends in a throw or carries a comment (whether it says why is review's to judge), and no `finally` returns, breaks or continues; a page change in a move is held to its anchored text (`tests/moved.js`); `tests/cleanup.js` holds charts, drag lists, intervals and WebGL contexts to what is on screen across repeated renders. Every export has a comment saying what it is for, and every module is formatted by Prettier (a data table, or the function that builds one, may keep its rows behind a `prettier-ignore` at the top level, with the reason above it; `tests/hygiene.js` prints each). Since r52 (phase 3b): the name `M` is the model's and nothing else's; `tests/moved.js` reads each function taking it back to r51's code on the syntax tree (`M.s` as `state`, `M.today` as `today`, `M.c.X` as the cache, `fIn(M, ...)` as `f(...)`) and refuses any other use of the model, applies the nine listed prunes TypeScript's noUnusedLocals needs and verifies each, holds `freshCaches`, `modelOf`, `LIVE` and `live` to exact code and checks, and the adapters to their one form and to the legacy script's imports; nothing behind the model's back: every function taking the model, and all it reaches, reads no clock, randomness, store, live model, page global or changing module value but the listed date formatters (`tests/hygiene.js`, rule 6, reading the code as written: `Date` only as `new Date(a date)`, `Date.UTC` or `Date.parse`, `Math` never as a value or `random`, no global read by a computed name, a formatter's `format` only called with a date written in place (not none, `undefined`, `null` or `void`) and never taken as a value, no module value written by name or by Object's own writers, none made at load by running a function; a value undefined only when it runs, a write through a local alias and a read built by reflection are beyond a reading of the code: `tests/engine.js` freezes every module table and watches the log for writes, and review reads the rest); the casts and `!` moved code needs count against its move's own number in `ARCHITECTURE.md`; a name imported from `live.ts` is shared state to `scripts/depmap.js`, and since r53 any module export that reads the store, the live model or the page, directly or through other modules (`moduleReach`, held by a fixture in `tests/hygiene.js`). Since r53 moved code changes after its move only through `edits` in `tests/moved.js`: per declaration, [was, now] text pairs that are undone before the token comparison with its move's baseline, each `now` found once in the declaration and once in its file, the names it newly uses stated and bound, and an executable check; a new declaration goes in `added` with its exact code, as before. New engine or analytics code takes the model as its first parameter, never reads `state` or the clock, and the legacy script reaches it through an adapter in `live.ts`. `tests/r52.js` compares r51 and r52 (`baselines/r52.html` since r53) on 1,000 wide histories, past midnight, stale caches and the demo, and `tests/r53w.js` r52 and this build the same way, each difference held to the r53 rule that makes it; `tests/engine.js` runs the engine alone in Node on frozen logs, with line coverage as a ratchet.
- **Code:** edit `src/`, then `npm run join`; never edit `index.html` by hand. Never reuse a class name the app has (`.tv`). Image tags in the script are written `\x3cimg`. Any new inline script, outside host or `eval` needs the Content Security Policy changed with it. Keep `window.__ironlog`. Baselines are never deleted.
- **Tests:** never weaken or delete an assertion to make it pass; a new behaviour gets a suite that fails on the build before it. Suites reach no outside service.

### Commands

```
npm install
npm run setup     # installs Chromium for Playwright (once)
npm run setup:webkit   # installs WebKit for Playwright (once)
npm test          # every suite, then the user-facing ones again on dist/, side by side (r44); must end with "All suites passed"
npm run build     # writes dist/ only
npm run join      # builds index.html from src/ (after any edit in src/)
node scripts/depmap.js   # the legacy script's size and dependency map (ARCHITECTURE.md)
npm run test:webkit    # every suite in WebKit (node tests/run.js --browser webkit); add -- --shard 1/4 or -- --jobs 1 as for npm test
```

Run a single suite with `node tests/<name>.js`, or `IRONLOG_BROWSER=webkit node tests/<name>.js` in WebKit. Each suite prints `ok`/`FAIL` lines, then `ALL PASS` (the fuzz suite prints `fuzz clean`).

The suites and what each checks: `tests/SUITES.md`.

### How the code is organised (search for these names)

- **New-install layout (r28):** `DEFAULT_HIDDEN` and `DEFAULT_ORDER` seed only a fresh state (`normalize(null)`: a new device, Erase everything, Sign out and remove). `normalize` reads a missing `autoDone` or `hidden` in an older save as the app behaved before (off, none), and adds `secOrder` after the other keys, as older builds do, so the synced settings keep one key order across builds (a different order made r21 and r28 phones rewrite each other's core; tests/r22.js caught it). `moreLine(tab)` ends a tab that has hidden sections (`secShow`, `goLayout`; `MORE_LIVE` skips sections that would be empty); `layoutDefault` and `layoutReset` go back to the defaults. Simple view (`SIMPLE_HIDE`) is gone. `demoKeep`/`demoRestore` (`KEY+'.demoSettings'`): demo data loaded on a first run puts the settings back when it is cleared.
- **State:** `freshState()`, `normalize()` and `STATE_VERSION`. `normalize()` runs on every load, import and cloud merge, and it validates every field. Since r43 every record it rebuilds goes through `keepExtra(src,out,known)`: a field this build does not know is kept as plain JSON (names `okExtraKey`, at most `EXTRA_KEYS` fields and `EXTRA_MAX` characters, names included, per record, never read), and a record that kept one takes the key order it came in, since sync documents are compared as written and a different order would make two builds rewrite each other on every pull (the r43 review), so an older phone no longer drops what a newer build added; `known` (`KNOWN_ITEM`, `KNOWN_CARDIO`, `KNOWN_MEAS`) names fields written only when valid, so an invalid one is dropped. An edit from History carries the session's and each exercise's unknown fields, exercises matched by lift in order (`ACT.finishNow`); the exercise editor works on a deep copy, so it keeps them. Phones on r42 or older still drop them, so a new field still needs every device updated before it is relied on. Any new field needs a default there. New optional fields stay `undefined` when unset, so existing record hashes don't change and cloud sync doesn't treat old records as edited. A migration is a `version < N` block that only adds data and never overwrites a value the user set.
- **Storage:** localStorage key `ironlog.v1`. **Cloud sync:** `cloudInit`, `cloudPull`, `cloudFlush`, `mergeChunk`, through `cloudProvider()` (Claude's db inside Claude, `window.ironlogCloud` in the standalone app). Data is split into `core`, `draft`, half-month session chunks `s-YYYY-MM-N` and, since r22, yearly body documents `b-YYYY` (bodyweights and measurements; `bodyChunkOf`), with a per-record three-way merge. Core still carries `bodyweights:[]` and `measurements:[]`, empty, in r21's key order, so an older build writes it back unchanged. Builds before r22 ignore `b-` documents and still write weigh-ins into core: `legacyBody`/`absorbLegacy` pick those up and move them; the first time a device sees a `b-` document its base comes from its pre-r22 core base (`legacyBodyBase`, `baseFor`), so deletes made on a device that updated first stay deleted. While an r21 phone is still in use, a delete or correction made on that phone is not applied on r22 phones (add-only pick-up); update every device.
- **Analytics:** `IDX()` builds everything once per change (`invalidate()` clears it): `byEx`, `exStats`, `weekSets`, `weekHard`, `dayMus`, `dayMeta`, `prs`, `scores`. Read from `IDX()` rather than recomputing, so the headline numbers and the detail sections can't disagree. Since r52 it is `IDXIn(M)` in `src/app/analytics.ts`, its cache the model's `_idx`, and the screens call it through `live.ts` as `IDX()`; the same holds for the rest of the engine and analytics (the 75 adapters since r53). Since r53 a step after the lighter readings, `idxTaggedFull` (2b), makes a lift done at its usual level on a deload or lighter day a full session for it.
- **Math:** `e1` (Brzycki up to 10 reps to failure, Epley at 11–12, none above 12; sets above RIR 4 excluded), `e1inv`, `rtfAt`, `rtfBand`. `calStats` and `rirEff` handle RIR calibration: shrink n/(n+3), cap ±2, 12-week window, compound vs isolation, applied only to sets at RIR ≤ 4. Also `suggest` (double progression), `progStep` and `jumpCap`, `recovery`, `rankDays`, `rankExercises`, `setSecs`/`setupSecs`/`dayMinutes`.
- **Load meaning:** `loadMode` (each / total / side / stack / bw; an assisted lift, `ex.assist`, is a bw lift whose sets store bodyweight minus the machine's help as a negative `w`: the field shows and takes the help as a plain number via `loadIn`, labels read BW−40, and `suggest` lowers the help to progress and raises it for a deload; `normalize` allows `w` down to −300 for this), `loadWords`, `loadNote`, `loadHead`, `lmChips`, `tonnage`. Every set stores the number exactly as typed. Estimates and targets stay in that frame, and tonnage counts what actually moved.
- **UI:** `render()` rebuilds `#view` from strings, with the views `viewToday`, `viewLogger`, `viewProgram`, `viewDash`, `viewHistory`, `viewSettings`. Since r57 the host in `src/app/view.ts` draws every tab, a moved screen as a lit-html template updated in place: History and Settings (r57), Plan, the library and Stats (r58, `plan.ts`, `stats.ts`, `graphs.ts`), Today, the week bar, the muscle map and the calendar (r59, `today.ts`, `week.ts`, `map.ts`, `calendar.ts`); the session screen stays a markup string until r60. `sec()` is the one foldable section component; every section starts folded each visit except Sessions on History (`MAIN_FIXED`, `openMain`). Since r28 a section's explanation is `helpLink` (How this works) as the first line inside it, never a ? in the header beside the arrow; folds inside a section (`.subsec`) sit on their own surface. Actions are `data-act="name"` handled in the `ACT` object. `renderModal`/`renderModalBody` draw the sheets and keep their scroll and focus across re-renders.
- **Look:** tokens on `:root` (light) and the dark blocks. The Today card uses `--hero`, `--on-hero`, `--hero-bg`, `--hero-ring` and `--hero-shadow`, light in the light theme since r21; the rest-timer bar uses `--dock`, `--on-dock` and `--dock-bg`, inked in both themes. `--volt` is the accent and means go, done and progress; `--volt-line` is its thin-line form. The accent is chosen in Settings > General (`ACCENTS`: Ember, the default since r37 (`ACCENT0`; nothing stored reads as Ember), Blue, Volt, Pink) and applied as `data-accent` on `<html>`, which swaps `--volt`, `--on-volt`, `--volt-line`, `--volt-soft` and, for Blue, turns "above target" (`--blue`) violet so it never reads as done. Gold (`--gold`) is only for PRs. Green, amber, blue and red keep their meanings. Dark is the default for new installs. The r20 design layer: `--btn`, `--field`, `--card-b`, `--glass`, `--hero-bg`; sections unboxed with sentence-case titles; frosted header and tabs.
- **Undo:** `snapshot(inv)` records the state before an action; the first save after it records the result. Since r22 the stack is kept in IndexedDB (`ironlog-undo`, `undoPersist`, `undoRestore` on boot, entries over 7 days dropped), so Undo in Settings works after the app is reopened; `inv` functions are not stored, those entries undo through the merge. `undoForget` clears it when a signed-out phone removes its log. `ACT.undo` runs `inv` when given (in-session actions: remove exercise, remove logged set, swap), else a three-way merge (per exercise for the session in progress, `mergeDraft`), so work logged after the action survives.
- **Targets per day's range (r27):** `suggest` reads the sessions logged in the exact rep range the plan asks for (`rr`), so a lift on a heavy and a light day progresses per day, while that range is in use (its newest session within 42 days of the newest full session); sessions with no range on record go with them. Otherwise, a range new or not used lately, it reads every full session (the newest wins). The Last line follows the same choice. `planFor(exId,R,dayId)` prefers the day being trained.
- **Well past the range (r27):** every top set 2+ reps over repMax (a range moved down) sets the load from the e1RM estimate for the middle of the range, capped by `jumpCap`, whole steps; since r31 `fitE` reads sets past 12 reps to failure with Epley up to 30 for this, so a set of 25 still sets the load. A load step of 0 is a fixed load (the at-cap reps branch, `capped` only at a real cap).
- **Grey reps after a new load (r27):** when `b.tgt.kind==='load'`, `greyR` skips last time's reps (they were at the old load) and uses the set above, else the target's repMin.
- **Steps and lighter loads (r27):** `stepRead`/`unitStep`: either unit's default (5 lb or 2.5 kg) reads as the current unit's; any other step as typed. `lighterLoad(ex,plan,from,to)` steps down from `from` in whole steps of `incOf` (never under the bar on a compound barbell lift typed as the total), used by deloads, warm-ups (inserted after the existing ones, climbing) and drop sets.
- **Changes reach the session (r27):** `refreshEx(exId)` (load step sheet, heaviest load, exercise editor) and `syncDraftPlan(f)` (a Plan item's range, RIR, rest or step, on the day being trained) re-read targets for exercises with no set done; typed loads are never touched. `swapPlan(p,from,to,R,dayId)` gives a swapped-in exercise its routine plan, or its own default when timed differs, else the slot's; set count kept.
- **Custom setup in the numbers (r27):** `ex.kind` ('compound'/'iso', the editor's Movement) overrides `autoCompound`. Stalls need the last session within 42 days of both today and the newest session, and a live, unarchived exercise. `muscleStatus` treats a 0 minimum as met. `planHard(it)` (RIR 3 or less, or RIR off) for planned hard sets; `planSetsPerWeek(R,deload)` halves per item. `weekRoutine(ws)`/`weekNeed(ws)` score a past week against the routine most of its sessions used (streak and adherence). Pick for me halves sets in deload and lighter sessions.
- **Last time and targets (r26, V's rule):** `lastOf(all,base,fmt)` builds the Last line from the most recent session with the exercise (in the day's range while it is in use, r27), whatever kind it was, and labels it trimmed, lighter or deload. Targets still come from the last full session (`suggest` skips cut, lighter and deload sessions, but since r53 not a lift done there at its usual load and reps for as many sets); when that is an earlier session, the block keeps its date in `basis` and the target shows a tip naming it. Last time's reps follow the shown session, except after a deload, whose reps were cut on purpose.
- **Last time's reps:** `lastRFor(b,si)` maps a row to last session's working sets (warm-ups and drop sets excluded); never index `lastR` by row.
- **Past workouts (r21):** `ACT.pastOpen` (an empty past day in the week bar, or History's Log a past workout) opens `pastSheet`: a date, then the routine day that was due (`pastDue`, from `cycleState(R,asOf)`, or the weekday) first, other training days, Freestyle. `pastDraft` builds a draft with `past:true`, `startedAt:null` and blocks from `newBlock(...,{before:date})`. `suggest(...,{before})`, `livePR(exId,s,date)` and `lastNoteOf(exId,before)` read only sessions up to that date (`statsUpTo` rebuilds the bests). Quick entry: no check-in, no rest timer or its buttons, typed reps tick the set. Changing a draft's date re-reads its targets (`refreshBlock`). Sessions sort by date everywhere, so one entered late lands in order.
- **Grey reps and Discard (r21):** `greyR(b,si)` is the number an empty reps field shows and the checkmark, auto-mark and the keyboard's Done take: last time's reps, else the working set above in this session, else the bottom of the planned range; `refreshGrey` updates it as reps are typed. `draftEntered(d)` says whether anything was put in (ticks, typed reps, cardio minutes, exercise notes, session notes other than `note0`, the text the app wrote); Discard (top and beside Finish) and replacing a session ask only then.
- **Logger flow:** a finished exercise folds to one line (`blockDone`, `autoFold`, `ui.blkOpen`); ticking done on an empty reps field logs last time's reps (`lastR`); RIR is a 0 to 5 strip (`rirSelect`, `rirShowStrip`, `ACT.rirPick`) that opens when the set's RIR box is tapped, and after each ticked set only when `settings.rirAsk` is on. Loads come prefilled from `suggest()`; with no history, a load typed on a working set fills the empty loads below it (`carryLoad`, and rows it filled follow a correction). Auto-mark (`settings.autoDone`, on for new installs since r28, off for older saves): typing reps and leaving ticks the set, and the first tap on that set's checkmark within 15 s (until another set is touched) confirms it rather than unticking it (`lastAuto`); leaving an empty reps field (Next, the iPhone keyboard's Done, a tap on blank space) accepts the grey number (`acceptGrey`, the `focusout` handler); moving within the row or tapping a button does not. `rebuilding` is true while `#view` is replaced, so the change the browser fires for a removed focused field never ticks a set. − Set on a row with reps typed, and removing a filled cardio entry, give an Undo; empty ones go quietly.
- **Updates (standalone app):** the service worker made by `scripts/build.js` precaches past the HTTP cache, and a new version waits instead of taking over. The build's boot script calls `ironlogUpdateReady()`, which shows `#updbar` ("New version ready", Reload, Later); Reload saves, then `ironlogApplyUpdate()` lets the waiting worker take over and the page reloads once. It also checks for updates whenever the app returns to the foreground.
- **Privacy:** `privacyLine()` in Settings > Your data says where the log lives and who can see it: this device, the Ironlog account (you and the app owner), or the Claude account inside Claude.
- **Bodyweight PRs (r32.1):** `prLoad(ex,s,date)` is the load PRs are judged at: on a bodyweight lift, the latest weigh-in times its share plus the added load, the same for every session; otherwise `loadOf`. Each point in `byEx` carries `pl`, and each exposure `pb`/`pbb` (the best estimate and its band at `pl`; equal to `best`/`bb` on a loaded lift). The PR list, `byBand`, `livePR`, `refreshPR`, `prBeat`, `allTimeBests` (records and heaviest) and the timed headline read those; estimates, trends, charts and `best`/`bestAll` keep each day's bodyweight.
- **A close variation's first session (r32.1):** `kinOf(ex,T)` finds the newest related lift (same free-weight `equip`, `loadMode`, `perSide`, one-sided by name `KIN_UNI`, `primary` and `patternOf`; not bw, assisted, timed or `noE1`) with a full session in the last 120 days; `kinStart` turns its working sets' `fitE` into a start (90%, 80% on a compound lift, middle of the range at the plan's RIR plus 1, down to the step or the related lift's `rackStep`). `suggestCore`'s no-history branch uses it (returns `kin`) before the first-time line.
- **What suggestions are (r31.1, V):** `SAFETY_TXT` (estimates from the lifter's log, not medical, physical therapy or coaching advice, can be wrong; technique, safety bars or a spotter, stop on pain, a doctor before starting or changing training; own risk) sits behind an i in three quiet places: `safetyLine()` under the privacy line in Settings > Your data, `#obSafety` on the first-run Set up page ("Suggestions are estimates, not advice.", short for r21's 45-character lines), and the first line of the Guide's How targets are set. Keep it short on screen; it is a notice, not legal advice, and a Terms page reviewed by a lawyer is V's call (PENDING 189).
- **Confirming with the code (r45, item 61):** `codeField(m)` on the waiting sheet and on sign-in after "Confirm your email first" or when the wait gives up (`m.needCode`); `ACT.acctCode` sends digits only to `ironlogAuth.verifyCode` (`verifyOtp`, type `email`; `signup` is deprecated there), owns the success path (the poll held off with `acctWait.busy`, the sign-in event's wait branch standing aside on `acctWait.coding`), and on any error tries the password once, since a link opened first uses the code up. `ACT.acctResend` → `resendCode` (`resend`, type `signup`, with the build's own redirect, so the preview's link returns to the preview). `friendly` gives a wrong, used or expired code one message (the service answers all three alike, `otp_expired`) and the once-a-minute limit as "Wait N seconds". Go in the code field confirms with the code (`acctSubmit` and the form's submit route it). Needs OWNER.md C6 (the code in the email template). The same lift again later in a session shows dashes in the Last column and the whole Last line, and `lastRFor` offers it no grey reps from last time (the set above or the range's bottom instead, no Copy last) (`lastDup`, keyed like `sameExOnce`), since how last time's sets split between the blocks is not recorded. `acctCode` waits for a poll already signing in before it tries the code, and gives up with "Still checking" if it is still running after 5 s. The r45 audit's fixes: the standalone worker answers only the app's own page with the app (any other path on the site, LICENSES.txt say, goes cache then network; tests/pwa.js opens it in a tab); a save this build cannot read is kept aside by `load()` and offered back in Settings > Your data (`unreadableRaw`, `ACT.unreadRestore` through `importData(text,after)`, `unreadShow`, `unreadForget`); records `normalize` drops at start are counted into Diagnostics (`log` after `load()`). `fitRecordOld` runs at every start before the first sync, so two devices on different routines could record different floors once; the later edit wins.
- **r45, V's 2026-10-13 list and the audit's leftovers:** auto-reorder: the first set ticked on an exercise below untouched ones (`blockUntouched`: no tick, every row `rowBlank`) moves it to just after the last one touched (`orderAt`, `orderSoon`/`orderRun` in the sections part; the block object is remembered, the move waits while a field is typed in, a strip or sheet is open, a drag or a fold is pending, and the block stays where it is on screen; `lastAuto`, `pendAuto` and the focused element's `data-b` followed the move on r45; since r46 only the `data-b` does, the rest holding the exercise's own id); never in a superset, a past workout or an edit, or past the same lift (`lastDup` reads repeats by order). Actual over planned: the hero ring is sessions this week of the plan's (`heroRing`, the same numbers as This week's title); the Stats tiles are Sessions and PRs (`big`) in the last 28 days with the 28 before when the log reaches back, then the average session; adherence moved into Consistency (`#adhLine`); hard sets against the plan stay in This week and Volume. The Guide's How targets are set opens with an In short block (before a session, during it, grey or filled loads). `full3t`, a trained 3-day full body (6 to 9 hard sets a week a major muscle, lighter than the defaults like the other 3-day ones). `draftNote(prev)` after a sync adopts another device's draft document: saved, replaced, or discarded on another device. `dropOnlyLift(id)` (last 6 sessions with drop sets and no main sets) keeps a lift out of `fatigueStalls`, read from the log so IDX keeps its shape (tests/r36.js compares it to r35's). `fbShrink` keeps a screenshot to 500,000 characters (1280, 960, then 720 px), since the tickets repository keeps every one. `docs` and `docs_preview` take at most 1 MB a document (`SUPABASE.md` Part 6, `OWNER.md` D7). `tests/seams.js`: random saves normalize the same twice, reassemble from their sync documents, and come back unchanged through the newest baseline; `tests/hygiene.js` checks that baseline exists and that every baseline is in `tests/migration.js`.
- **r46, the r45 quality check:** `recent4w()` is the one window for sessions and PRs (`big`): the last 28 days, today included, and the 28 before only when the first session is on or before day 56 (`back`); Today's PR tile, the Stats tiles and Recent PRs read it. `weekSessions(ws)` reads a per-week count built once per change (kept in IDX on r46, in `dateIndex` since r47); adherence counts sessions with it (a two-a-day is two, as the ring, This week and the streak count). A rest week has no ring and This week says Rest week. `cloudFlush` sends the draft document last, so Finish's session is up before the emptied draft (`draftNote` said discarded in between); an edit replaced from another device says so. `friendly`'s wait message is "Wait N seconds, then try again." (Create account and Reset password show it too). `fmtDate`/`fmtDateLong` use kept `Intl.DateTimeFormat`s (`dtf`), the same output. `draftNote` says only what it can see (the session it became, the workout that replaced it, else closed): whether a workout it cannot see was finished or discarded is recorded nowhere, and the other phone's session document may not have arrived, so r45's "discarded" came out wrong on a save that failed to upload. **Every exercise in a session has an id (r46):** `newBlock` and the History edit give each block a `bid`, and `normalize` keeps a valid one but never invents one (it also runs in the sync comparison, where a value made here would differ per device); a draft from an older build has none. `blkRef(bi)`/`blkIdx(ref)` in the adjust part are the one way to point at an exercise over time: the id, then the object, then the place with the lift checked, so a session started on r45 behaves as it did. `lastAuto`, `pendAuto` and `autoFold`'s timer hold a `blkRef`, `moveBlock` no longer clears `lastAuto`, and `orderRun` renumbers only the focused field's `data-b`, which is markup. A place survives a rebuild but not a move (on r45 a move dropped the confirm window, so the checkmark tapped right after an auto-marked set unticked it); the object survives a move but not a rebuild, and `normalize` rebuilds every block on each sync, Undo, cross-tab write and restore. `tests/seams.js` generates `bid` so the cross-build check proves r45 keeps it (`keepExtra`) and writes the documents back unchanged. Still keyed by position, not migrated: `ui.modal.b` (sheets; `orderSoon` defers while a sheet is open, but a sync can rebuild under one), `lastAuto.s`/`pendAuto.s`/`b.adj.at` (set rows), `blkKey` and `carried` (identity, so they lapse harmlessly after a rebuild).
- **The screens against each other (r46):** `tests/coherence.js` asks the one question no other suite asked, whether two places showing the same quantity show the same number. It states no new rules and compares the app to itself, it runs on a published build too (`node tests/coherence.js baselines/r45.html`), and 8 of its checks fail on r38 through r44, 7 on r45. Every finding of the r45 quality check was of this shape, and every one of those builds was green. Extend it when a release adds or moves a number a lifter reads.
- **Rest weeks on Today (r46):** `weekStats()` reports `rest`, with `planDays` and `tgt` at 0, so the ring, the card's title and its body all follow one test instead of each making its own (r45 showed the ring and "0 hard sets logged of 145 planned" through a rest week).
- **Waiting for confirmation (r21):** after sign-up the account sheet is in mode `wait`: `waitStart` keeps the email and password in memory only (`acctWait`) plus a marker without the password (`KEY+'.acctWait'`), and `waitTry` signs in on returning to the front, on "I have confirmed", and every 20 s (60 s after 5 minutes, stops at 30). Closing the sheet forgets it. A confirmation link opened in a browser asks `where` (`ACT.acctWhere`: `app` signs that browser out, `here` calls `ironlogAuth.release()` and syncs); no answer signs it out; if the marker is in that storage it signs in without asking.
- **Feedback (r21, reworked r25):** `fbSheet`, `ACT.fbOpen` (the menu at the top right, an exercise's ⋯ menu), `fbContext` (what is attached: never workouts), `fbShrink` (screenshot to a 1280 px JPEG), `ACT.fbSend` through `window.ironlogFeedback` (defined in `scripts/cloud-supabase.js`), or a copy of the report where there is none. `acct.reader` (from `fbCheckReader`) shows the owner's Inbox (`fbInboxSheet`). See `FEEDBACK.md`.
- **Accounts (standalone app):** the app only looks for `window.ironlogAuth` (`AUTH()`) and `window.ironlogCloud`; `scripts/cloud-supabase.js` defines both. `acctInit`, `acctPanel`, `acctSheet` (modes signin, signup, forgot, newpw, sent), `ACT.acctSubmit`, `ACT.acctSignOut` (since r33: Sign out removes the phone's copy once synced, or keeps it by the quiet link; then the first page), `cloudStop`. Signed out, the account is offered on the first-run welcome card and as a slim line on Today (`acctBar`, hidden per device by Not now). Sync notes carry the account id (`cloud.acct`): a different account on the same device starts over as a first link. An emailed link opened in a browser (not the installed app) is handled there, then that browser is signed out again so it never uploads what it holds. Accounts are optional; without one nothing changes. The emailed sign-in link option was left out on purpose: on an iPhone it opens in Safari, whose storage is separate from the home-screen app.
- **Breached passwords (r26):** Supabase's Leaked Password Protection is a Pro plan feature, so `scripts/cloud-supabase.js` checks itself: `breached(password)` sends the first 5 characters of the password's SHA-1 hash to Have I Been Pwned's range API (with `Add-Padding`), and `signUp` and `updatePassword` refuse a match with `code:'breached'`. Any error or a wait over 3 s lets the password through, so an outage never blocks sign-up.
- **Standalone app:** `isStandalone()` switches export to the share sheet (Save to Files) and the storage wording. `cloudProvider()` takes Claude's db inside Claude, else `window.ironlogCloud` (`{db, userId}` with the same doc/collection calls), and sync, merging and chunking work unchanged.
- **Comeback (former bests):** removed in r19 at the owner's request, with the saved former bests: `normalize()` deletes `priors` from every load, import and merge.
- **Back gesture:** `navSync()` (called from `render()`) adds a history entry per tab change; `popstate` closes a sheet first, else shows the entry's tab (`ilTab`). A sheet's leftover entry is replaced by the next tab change.
- **First run (r22):** a new install opens on its own pages instead of Today (`obActive`, `obView`; `body.obmode` hides the header and tabs). Page 1, only where accounts exist: what the app does, then Save your log (Create account, I have an account, Continue without an account; `OB_ACCT` per device, `ACT.obAcctLater`/`obAcctBack`). Page 2, Set up: optional name, units (`ACT.obUnit`), then Pick a ready-made routine, Log a workout now, Build my own routine, Import a backup (`#importFile`), and Look around with sample data. Any session, demo data, a draft, or a sign-in that brings a log ends it; while the device is still finding out, a splash shows instead. `welcomeCard` now only shows the demo note.
- **PRs (r22):** the PR feed records what each PR beat (`prev` for a best estimate, `prevR` for a rep PR). A PR set gets a gold toast with that (`prBeat`, `toast(...,'pr')`), a gold pulse on the row (`.prflash`, off with reduced motion) and a buzz where allowed; the recap opens with a gold block (`#recapPR`). Stats > Lifts > All-time bests (`bestsBoard`, `allTimeBests`): per lift the best estimate, the heaviest set, and the rep records (most reps at each load where no heavier set did as many, with the date first reached), newest record first; six lifts show, Show all (`ui.bestsAll`) lists the rest.
- **Importing (r23):** `impStart` reads a file or pasted text: an Ironlog backup goes to `importData` (restore), a table (`impRows` CSV/TSV, `impXlsx` via DecompressionStream) goes through `impHeader` (`IMP_COLS`, best header first) and `impFromTable` (Strong, Hevy, hand-made sheets, Ironlog's CSV), anything else through `impFromText` (notes, built on `parseSets` with `impSetsText`). `openPreview` matches names (`impMatch`: `impNorm` synonyms, `IMP_ALIAS`, equipment and modifier words) and `impSheet` shows counts, the unit, exercise choices (or a new custom exercise with a muscle), what was read, lines not understood, skipped rows and duplicates. `impBuild` makes freestyle sessions in kg and skips ones already in the log; `ACT.impGo` saves through `normalize` with Undo. Nothing is saved before the preview.
- **Header (r23):** the wordmark left the bar (`.vh` keeps it for screen readers); the days touch, so each is at least 44 px wide from 375 px phones up. This week owns this week (sessions in the header, hard sets against planned, weight lifted in `liftedLine`); At a glance keeps the streak and PRs, whose tile opens All-time bests.
- **Name and routines:** `settings.userName` (optional, 40 characters) showed a greeting on Today until r69, when the greeting and the Name field went (V, 2026-11-03); a name stored is kept by `normalize` and nothing asks or shows it. `TEMPLATES` holds the ready-made routines (full body 3, full body 3 trained `full3t` since r45, upper/lower 4, PPL 6, body-part split 5, dumbbells 3, and the starter high-volume routine); `routineFromTemplate()` copies one with new ids. The sheet opens from the welcome card (replaces the untouched starter) and from Plan (adds, and opens the existing copy instead of a duplicate, by `routineSig`).
- **3D body:** `B3`, `mount3D`, `map3dLoad` (imports three.js from `THREE_URLS`), `B3_PARTS` shapes; each muscle region is shaded by this week's sets and picked by raycast. The flat map stays.
- **Install:** `siteHost()`/`siteDir()` put the address the app is on into the pictures and steps (r27). `installBar`, `installSheet`, `installPic`, `ACT.install`, `installLater`. The build's head script sets `window.IRONLOG_APP` and captures `beforeinstallprompt`. `installKind()` tells Safari 26 (Share behind ⋯ beside the address), older Safari, Chrome and other iPhone browsers apart; iPhones get "Add to Home Screen", a picture of where to tap and two steps, with the reasons in a tip. On Today the account line comes first, Install second.
- **Menu (r27):** redrawn only when the save state or account changes (`setSaveState.last`). The header's round button (`#saveState`, `data-act="menuOpen"`, `MENU_ICON` with a status dot from `saveStateNow`; tinted with the accent and ringed since r28, still 34 px so the week bar's days stay 44 px wide at 375 px) opens `menuSheet`: account (sign in, create, or the email and Sign out), sync state, Backup due, Report a problem, the owner's Reports inbox, Guide, Show, hide or reorder sections (r28), Your data and backups, and the version people see (since r49; the build is in Diagnostics). Settings has the same Guide and reports in its Help group (r28).
- **Guide (r27):** `guideSheet` (kind `guide`, `ACT.guideOpen`, topics today, log, rules, stats, plan, sections, data). It states the rules the code applies; change it with them.
- **Status bar strip (r28):** `body::before` covers the safe area above the sticky header (dark in the light theme in the installed app, whose status bar text is white), so scrolled content no longer shows behind the clock.
- **Starter routine (r29, decision 114):** `starterRoutine()` is the ul4 template for a new install (`freshState`, an empty routines list); `starterSigs()` matches it and the pre-r29 six-day one, so a first-run pick replaces either untouched. `window.IRONLOG_STARTER` picks another; only `tests/h.js` sets it (to `onemuscle`, which most suites were written on); `opts.realStarter` runs as shipped.
- **Targets that read right (r29):** `belowMin(v,lo)`/`aboveMax(v,hi)` compare at one decimal, everywhere a flag is decided. `muscleStatus(src,soFar)`: no sets is `none` (on an average with a minimum above 0, `under`), the week in progress short of the minimum is `part` (`--part`, pale green); `lowSt`. `regionBalance` gives `pct` against the minimum and `low`/`high`; the radar draws at most 200% (`RCAP`, real share in the tooltip). `VOL_MODES` (no Planned on Stats). Muscle scores label a flat muscle lagging only with a stalled lift (`stall`).
- **Demo (r29):** `makeDemo` follows each item's double progression (a level per lift and range, a real step after every set made the top, costing about a rep per 2.5%; bodyweight lifts with no load climb every other session), every planned session to yesterday, loads never under the bar. `demoLoad` on a first run fits targets; `demoRestore` puts them back.
- **PRs (r29):** `Weight PR` (more weight than any earlier set, as logged) comes before `e1RM` and `Rep PR`, one per lift and session; it keeps `prevW` and, when it also beat the estimate, `value`/`prev`. `livePR` checks it before the load guard. Shown as Weight PR, Best estimate, Rep PR.
- **Rest timer look (r29):** `--dock`/`--dock-bg` are tints of `--volt` in both themes with white text; `--tp` drives the bar along the top (`timer.total`, saved beside `TIMER_KEY` as `TIMER_KEY+'Len'`).
- **Plates (r29):** `settings.smallPlates` (absent unless on) adds 1.25 lb or 0.5 kg (`plateList`); `ex.barKg` (absent unless set) is a lift's own bar (`exBarKg`), for `plateText` and the `lighterLoad` floor.
- **Content Security Policy (r29):** `scripts/build.js` puts a meta policy in both builds: inline scripts by sha256 (computed last), connect only to this site, the Supabase project and Have I Been Pwned. Any new inline script, outside host, or `eval` needs the policy changed with it. Suites that edit the built page run it through `cspFix` (`tests/h.js`). Image tags in the script are written `\x3cimg`: Chromium's look-ahead scanner now and then reads the inline script as markup and fetched `${m.shot}` as an image.
- **Lists in sentences (r29):** `andList(items,max)` writes "a and b", "a, b, and c", or "a, b, c, and 2 more"; written copy uses the serial comma too (tests/r29.js scans every tab, tip and the Guide).
- **Reordering a session (r29):** `bgrip(bi,name)` puts a ⠿ handle on each exercise (open or folded); `mountBlockSortable` (Sortable on `#view`, handle `.bgrip`, draggable `section.block`) and the arrow keys on the handle move it through `moveBlock(a,b)`, the same path as the ⋯ menu's `bUp`/`bDown`: it clears `foldT` and `lastAuto` (both point at a block by position) and runs `fixSupersets` so a split pair is no longer a superset. `blockDrag` is true during a drag, so `autoFold` waits and the focused field is blurred first. The handle looks 26 px wide; `::before` widens its touch to 44 px. The `focusout` guard treats `[role="button"]` like a button, so leaving an empty reps field for the handle accepts nothing. While dragging, `body.blkdrag` shows each exercise (and Sortable's floating copy) as one line, with the scroll adjusted so the held one stays under the finger (room added below the list when the shorter page cannot scroll that far), `dragOpenUp` moving the scroll with the exercises as they open at the drop, and `edgeScroll` scrolls the page near the header or the tab bar (Sortable's own scrolling did not run for a touch drag on the window). `mergeDraft` keeps the session's current order when the action being undone did not change it.
- **Tips:** any element with `data-tip` shows it on mouse hover (350 ms), keyboard focus, or a touch hold (480 ms; the click that follows is swallowed). `tipi(text)` makes a small "i" button that shows its tip on a tap. Since r27 `tipLabels` (a MutationObserver) gives every `label .hastip` an i button beside it (and names the control by the label text with `aria-labelledby`, since a button in a label spoils its name), and other `.hastip` text shows its tip on a tap. Long explanations go in tips or the Guide, not on screen.
- **Copy my log from Ironlog (preview only, r29):** the preview's log starts empty, so `ACT.previewCopy` (Settings > Your data, both first-run pages, the empty radar) copies the real log in: `liveLogFetch` reads the live app's `ironlog.v1` on this phone and, signed in, the account's live rows through `window.ironlogLiveDocs` (defined only in the preview build of `scripts/cloud-supabase.js`, read-only), and takes the one with more sessions. An iPhone keeps each home-screen app's storage apart, so there the account copy is the one found. It asks before replacing a preview log, keeps no draft, and has Undo; the live app and its table are never written.
- **Preview build (r24):** `node scripts/build.js --preview` sets `window.IRONLOG_ENV='preview'`, and `ENV` in the app. It shares the live app's origin (ironapp.org since 2026-10-02; vqx7.github.io/Ironlog forwards there), so everything stored is kept apart by name: `KEY` is `ironlog-preview.v1` (every other key derives from `KEY`), the undo database `ironlog-preview-undo`, the sign-in slot `ironlog-preview.auth` and the sync table `docs_preview` (in `scripts/cloud-supabase.js`), and caches start `ilpreview-` (the live worker clears every other `ironlog-` cache). The live worker leaves navigations under `preview/` to the network, so the preview page can load and install its own worker (scope `preview/`). Name "Ironlog Preview", home-screen label "Preview", striped icon, a labelled PREVIEW tag on the tab bar (r25; the striped edges read as stray dotted lines), "Ironlog Preview" in Settings. No Delete my account in the preview (it would delete the real account). Feedback context carries `env`.
- **Swap and suggestions (r24):** a swap takes its set count from the rows the exercise has now, not the routine's plan, and keeps `cut`. Each block stores the load the app suggested (`sw`, null when none; absent in drafts from before r24); `refreshBlock` (a date change, a new heaviest load) replaces only rows still on that suggestion, so a typed load is never overwritten.
- **Grey loads (r25):** a new row's load is `null` and the field shows `greyW(b,si)` in grey: the load of the working set above it (typed, or taken when ticked), else the block's suggestion `b.sw`. `settleLoad` writes the grey load into the row when the set is ticked (`setDone`), and Finish does the same for sets with reps but no tick, so a saved set never has an empty load. `effW` is the typed or grey load; warm-ups, drops, plates, + Set and Type or dictate sets read it. `settings.loadFill==='fill'` (Settings > Rest timer and logging > "Fill in suggested loads"; unset means grey) fills rows as before, with `carryLoad`. Typed loads are never overwritten. From r26 to r34 the first session asked (`loadAskCard`, removed in r35); since then only the Settings switch does, and `ACT.loadAsk` converts the session in progress both ways: fill writes the grey numbers into blank rows, grey empties rows still on the suggestion or carried down.
- **Logo (r25):** an upright dumbbell that is also the I of Ironlog, amber plates (`--brand`) on a handle in `--brand-bar`. `scripts/icons.py` draws the icons; `MARK_SVG` and `LOCKUP` in the app draw the same numbers for the first-run pages and the loading screen. The class names are `mk-p` and `mk-h` (`.hb` is the help button). `iconBar`/`iconSheet`: an iPhone home-screen install that had a log before r25 (`HAD_LOG`, read before any save) is told once how to get the new icon, with a warning that removing the app removes the log on the phone unless signed in (`KEY+'.icon25'`).
- **Problem report safety (r29 audit):** report rows are written by other people, through the API as well as the app. The Inbox shows a screenshot only when it matches `FB_SHOT_RE` (base64 JPEG from start to end) and escapes it; the B1 SQL checks the same pattern, and `feedback_limit` sets `created_at` and `issue_url` itself, so a back-dated row counts toward the day and no sender sets a ticket link.
- **Problem reports (r25):** see Feedback above; signed in only, Problem, Question or Suggestion (`FB_CATS`, stored as bug, question, idea), 10 to 1,000 characters (`FB_MIN`, `FB_MAX`), one image up to 10 MB (`FB_IMG_MB`), 5 a day (`FB_DAY`, counted in `KEY+'.fbSent'` and enforced by the table's trigger). The menu at the top right (r27; Settings no longer has it) and an exercise's ⋯ menu. `ACT.fbCopy` copies the details for someone signed out.
- **Volume and the radar (r28, r29):** Volume opens on By region (`ui.volMode='region'`); `regionSrc()` is the one source for the radar and its table: the recent average, else this week so far, else the last trained weeks however long ago (dated, with a note), else nothing; the chart always draws, empty against the target ring with nothing logged (V, r29: a card without the chart read as the chart being gone). Never the routine's plan (V, r29); Plan's Weekly volume and the Planned chip show the plan. `regionBalance` leaves tracked-only muscles out of both sides. `actualAvg4` and adherence start at `firstFullWeek()`: the week the log began counts only if it began on that week's first day. On a first run, `fitTargets(R)` (called from `tplPick` from the welcome pages) lowers each minimum to the routine's planned whole sets, only while the targets are all still `BANDS` or still fitted to a starter (r31). Muscles flags only what was logged, injuries and priorities, and before any session the subtitle says after a few sessions. Inside Lifts the folds are Recent PRs, All-time bests, Stalls. `weekCard` titles itself This month (or the month's name) in Month view.
- **Month calendar (r25):** `calSheet` (kind `cal`, `m.ym`), `ACT.calOpen`, `calMonth`, `calDay` (a day with sessions opens the first in History through `wkOpen`, which now loads enough History rows to reach it; an empty past day opens `pastSheet`; today opens Today). Opened from History's Calendar button and a Consistency square (past days are buttons). `calBody(ym,inline)` draws the grid for both the sheet and, since r26, the Month view inside This week on Today (`weekBody`, Week/Month toggle `ACT.wkView`, kept per device in `WKVIEW_KEY`, month in `ui.calYm`). The r25 pull-down on the header and its handle were removed in r26 (the month opened from the bottom, which made no sense); `--head-h` is 52 px again (62 px from 620 px).
- **Guardrails and blank rows (r25):** `rowBlank(b,x)` is a row with nothing of the lifter's (not ticked, no reps, no RIR, a load that is empty, the suggestion or carried down). − Set, Short on time and a swap take only blank rows; a filled row, an exercise with logged or typed sets, and cardio, weigh-in and tape entries ask before they go (`confirmBox`, resolved again by id when confirmed), with Undo after. Short on time records what it took for an exact Undo (`inv`). `keepOpen(fn)` carries each block's open state across moves, removals and a swap's insert (`blkOpenObj` remembers the block itself). `draftEntered` counts a typed load.
- **Owner actions:** `OWNER.md` lists every step V does by hand, with the exact code; `tests/owner-doc.js` keeps its code identical to the repo's. Update it whenever a build adds or finishes a step for V.
- **Most common lifts (r29.5, r33):** `COMMON` (library ids; since r33 the 30 lifts in StrengthLog's 2025 top 25 by sets logged, overall, for men or for women, shown as Most common), `isCommon(e)` (not custom, not marked less common), `exTags(e)`, `commonFirst(list)`; `libFiltered(q,m,e,kind)` takes `''`, `'common'` or `'rare'` (`ui.libKind`, `ACT.libKind`). Nothing is stored. `COMMON_TIP` says where the list comes from.
- **Target RIR (r29.5):** `RIR_TGT_TIP` (Plan editor) and the Guide's Plan topic explain the default of 1 (`DEFAULT_PLAN`) as a convention.
- **The retired Claude artifact (r29.5, code removed in r46):** the app also shipped as a Claude artifact until 2026-09-27 (`OWNER.md`). The features that ran only there (debrief, Ask Claude, suggested swaps, Claude reading typed sets) were marked `/*ai{*/ ... /*}ai*/` and stripped from both builds; in r46 they left `src/` too, the shipped builds byte-identical before and after. `scripts/build.js` and `tests/r29c.js` (on the page and, since r49, the source files with their comments) still fail on their names or a marker, so none of it returns by accident. The storage path (`window.claude.use('db')`) stays: it is shared code.
- **Training blocks and honest trends (r30, from the independent review):** `IDX()` reads a lift's session as lighter (`x.auto`) when its heaviest load is 10% or more under the heaviest of its previous three full sessions in that range and its reps-alone reading (`tRaw`) 7% or more under theirs; a week where at least half the lifts trained (and two or more) read lighter is a lighter week (`lightWk`, also every session tagged deload or lighter). Lighter sessions in a row for 2 weeks or more are a new level (`keep`) and count again (r31; three sessions read a deload week on a lift trained 3 times a week as a new level). Auto-lighter sessions are treated like tagged ones everywhere (`suggest`, trends, stalls, `lastOf` labels them lighter, `readiness`); `actualAvg4` skips lighter weeks and reaches up to 8 weeks back to replace them (only lighter weeks in reach: those). Each session has `tAdj` (RIR as read elsewhere) and `tRaw` (reps alone), both from `trendE` (e1 up to 12 reps to failure, Epley to 20, never shown as a 1RM); a lift's trend uses `tAdj` only when RIR is rated on 80% or more of its sets in its last 8 sessions in range (`useAdj`), else `tRaw` (`x.tv`). The 6-week window ends at each lift's own last session (`exWin`). A stall also needs no PR in the last 3 sessions and the load no heavier than at the start of the stretch. `pLo`/`pHi`: the weekly rate the 80% interval allows, shown as "about +1.5% a week (likely +1.2% to +1.8%)" (on the rate, not the 6-week change, so the endpoint percentage can never fall outside its own range). Muscle scores leave out lifts inside the noise; a muscle with none clear has `clear:false` and reads unclear (`musTrend`), never 0%. The coach suggests no deload within 4 weeks of a lighter week (`recentLighter`).
- **PRs and bests (r30):** a rep PR counts only at 90% or more of the strongest set before it (`REP_PR_SHARE`, by the same score), in the list and live (`livePR`). `bestsBoard` dates each best (estimate, heaviest) itself; a 1RM shows for compound lifts only (`exHeadline` shows `bestSet` on isolation lifts; `liftTrend` shows top sets). `prBeat` names the rep band (`BAND_WORDS`). Weight lifted uses the bodyweight known on or before the session (`bwKnownAt`) and is never shown as a percentage against last week. `sessionLength` needs 3 timed sessions.
- **Lighter day and the check-in (r30):** a lighter day is a one-day deload (`suggest` with `opts.light`: about 90% loads in whole steps, 3+ RIR, no target), passed wherever a block is made. `readyCard` under the check-in: two or more low answers offer Make it lighter (`ACT.makeLight`: since r31 only the exercises not started become lighter, `b.light`, with half their sets and new suggestions, with Undo; the session is not marked) and Short on time.
- **Finish (r30):** sets left undone ask first (`d._leftAsked`); an exercise stopped with fewer than half its sets done is saved as trimmed (`cut`); the recap counts targets only for exercises started and plans minutes from the session as trimmed (`draftPlanMin`). A real session saved while demo data is loaded removes the demo and says so in the recap.
- **Import and cardio (r30):** "OHP 3x8 40" is 3 sets of 8 at 40 (`impSetsText`). A table row with a distance, or a cardio name (`impCardioKind`) and no reps, becomes a cardio entry per date and kind (`cardioRows`, built in `impBuild`, saved by `impGo`). Cardio has `dist` in km, optional (`distU`, `distShow`, `distIn`: miles when logging in lb). `suggest` sets a new range's first load from the estimate (`!inRR`) and the rep target counts the plan's sets (`tp`).
- **Plan (r30):** a rep minimum typed above the maximum raises it (and the reverse). `plan.jump` (Load jump %, absent unless set, 0 to 20) overrides the 2.5% or 5% in `progStep` (`jumpPct`), copied by swaps and `syncDraftPlan`. `planEvidenceNote` on Plan's Weekly volume names muscles the plan puts under the default minimum where the target was brought down to meet it (V's choice: never a flag, never on Stats).
- **Joints and swaps (r30):** injuries take a joint (`JOINTS`, keys `j_knee`, `j_hip`, `j_shoulder`, `j_elbow`, `j_wrist`; `exJoints(e)` by pattern and main muscle; `injLabel`). `HARD_EX` lifts lose 4 in `swapScore` unless done before near the rep range (`canDo`); Limited equipment gives a hard swap 3 to 6 reps at 2 RIR and says so; its sheet is fixed height so the chips stay put.
- **Routines and lifts (r30):** `full3` is a beginner plan (5 exercises a day, machines and dumbbells, 3 RIR); `full2` (2 days) and `str5` (5 x 5, alternating) are new; `split5` spreads lateral raises over two days; `onemuscle` is trimmed to 11 sets per muscle per session with no neck circuit; `legacy` (hidden, `defaultRoutine()`) is what the suites run on (`tests/h.js`) and what pre-r29 installs started with. New lifts: sumo deadlift, pause squat, paused bench, floor press, power clean, kettlebell swing. `howToLink` (exercise menu and editor) opens a YouTube search for the name.
- **Demo (r30):** one marked deload week three weeks back and one missed day six weeks back (outside the volume average, so nothing reads below target).
- **Second review (r31):** `workLoad(pts,repMin)` is a session's working load (the load done for the most sets, the heavier on a tie, among loads at 60%+ of the session's heaviest total moved, bodyweight share included); grey reps (`lastOf`) are last time's reps at that load only, so a pyramid's lighter rows never set reps at the top load; `suggest`, `lastOf` (grey reps), IDX's `topW`/`atTop` and the trend readings use it, so a top single counts only for bests and PRs. `tAdj`/`tRaw` average the working sets. `tagged(x)` (deload, light, cut) and `offSession(x)` (also auto) are the one test for sessions that never set targets or trends. A trend needs 3 sessions over 14+ days (`spanW` for the wording); a small rise or fall whose interval excludes 0 reads "slowly up/down". Flat for a stall is under 0.1% a week (rounding dust hid identical lifts). The stall test also counts more reps at the same working load over no more sets as movement. `fitE` (e1, then Epley to 30) only for picking loads; `jumpCap(ex,load,early)` allows 25% in a lift's first 3 sessions. Make it lighter marks blocks (`b.light`, saved on the session's exercise, kept by `normalize` and edits), never the session. `calDueIdx` skips deloads, lighter days, past workouts and 2+ low check-in answers. `fatigueStalls` (compound lifts with an estimate) is what counts toward "stalled at once". `actualAvg4` counts `_missed` weeks; `avgWeeksTxt` words the average; the coach says missed weeks first and names a lift the lifter does. `routineFromLog` builds a routine from a first-run import in `ACT.impGo`: days by name, or by lifts in common (Jaccard 0.5) when names are generic (`GENERIC_WORKOUT`), contents from each day's latest 3 full sessions (RIR only when most sets were rated), `dayFor` links sessions, and `fitTargets(R,actual)` keeps no minimum above the log's own average; else the templates sheet opens with `imported`. `fitTargets` also refits targets still fitted to a starter. `repSpan`/`rangeWords` write one-number ranges. `toastQuiet` at Start session. After a load step that is large for the load (a dumbbell, a small stack), the new-load target is what the estimate allows (`aim`, saved as `tgt.repMin`), and the below-range drop waits while the reps rise or just after one step up (`justUp`, `rising`), so the load never swings between two numbers (r31.1, found by tests/realism.js). Library search matches words with hyphens and spaces ignored (`nameMatch`). The import fingerprint ignores loads.
- **First page and the radar's weeks (r31.2 and r31.3, V):** the account page (`obView`, step `acct`) opens with the wordmark and, since r33, the tour (see First page tour), then Save your log with one short line on what the account is for ("Back up your log and use it on any device"), then Continue without an account as a plain link (`.ob-later`, `ACT.obAcctLater`), not a third button; the page fits 375 x 667. The sign-up sheet says the email is only for signing in and never shared; both privacy tips and the Guide's Your data add that the owner can see the log and the email. `avgWeeks(n,lightToo)` is the weekly average over the last n full weeks (`actualAvg4` is `avgWeeks(4)`, which every flag, the coach and Muscles read). The By region radar averages 2, 4 or 8 weeks (`REGION_WKS`, `regionWk()`, kept per device in `KEY+'.regionWk'`, `ACT.regionWk`), 4 unless changed; fewer full weeks than chosen says "N full weeks logged so far", none yet falls back to this week so far and says why (`noFull`). The choice moves the radar and its table only, and the chips hide when no full week is logged (they would change nothing). `avgWeeks` keeps `_span` (how far back the weeks came from), `_light` (lighter weeks left out) and `_ff` (cut short by the first full week), so `avgWeeksTxt` and the radar say "the 3 weeks you trained of the last 5, a lighter week left out" rather than "3 full weeks logged so far". Ironlog's own CSV reads back with its `day` column as the workout name and its `deload` column (`impHeader`, only for our header, since other sheets' Day can be a date or weekday).
- **Third review (r32):** `suggest()` is a wrapper: a Lighter day or deload (`opts.light`/`opts.deload`) is worked out from `suggestCore()`'s normal prescription (90% of `min(target, top)` in whole steps, `lastR` at repMin); every `suggestCore` result carries `top` (last working load). `incOf` returns `rackStep(ex)` for dumbbell and kettlebell lifts (`rackKind`, `RACK`) with no step set on the exercise: the largest rack spacing every logged load fits, in the unit on screen, cached per IDX. `repFloor(pts,repMin)` reads one set under the range among two or more at repMin (grey reps in `lastOf`, rep targets in `suggestCore`, said in the text); a whole session under the range at a load done in the range the time before asks for repMin in every set again (a `load` target at the same load), and four sessions in a row under it drop the load (`shortRun`). A step is large when it costs 1.5 reps or more by the estimate on a range 3 or more reps wide (`wide`; a fixed-rep plan like 5 x 5 always steps); then `suggestCore` keeps the load and returns `buildTo` (reps to reach, at most a quarter of repMax past it), else steps with `aim` (floor, planned RIR). Bodyweight ab work with no load ever logged (`noAdd`) progresses by reps. Trends: `movers()` carries `wk`, muscle scores need lifts with 4 sessions over 21 days and carry `wk`; stalls need the newest session to beat every earlier one at its load (`same`), and their slope is the Theil-Sen median of pairwise slopes (`sen`), so one good or bad day does not decide it; `liftTrend` follows the lift's trend status and says no direction without a trend. `snapUnit` puts a suggestion on the step in the unit on screen when the last working load is off it (a log kept in the other unit). `oddReps`/`oddOk`: reps over 30 and twice the range or the lift's most ever ask before a set counts. `routineFromLog` repeats the days when each came round twice or more a week. PRs carry `big` (stronger than ever by the rep-PR score, or no estimate): `prsWithin`, Recent PRs and its count, the recap and `livePR` use only those; the recap names other heavier sets as "Heaviest yet". Deload and lighter sessions recorded no PRs until r53 (V, 2026-10-18: a PR is a PR whenever it happens); since then they do, the ones stronger than ever (`big`) counted and shown at Finish, though not announced as the set is logged (`refreshPR` skips lighter and deload work: legacy code, phase 5). Live, a set no stronger than an earlier set in the same session is not announced (`refreshPR`). `impSetsText` reads a bare "@6" to "@10" as RPE when the line has a load. The muscle row for a stalled lift with no clear trend reads "A lift stalled". `impSetsText` reads RPE (to RIR), `+10` and `BW` before sets. `minTxt` rounds to 5. Finish snoozes a passed-over RIR check (`calSnooze`). `exHistSheet` (kind `exhist`, `ACT.bHist`) and `bwAskCard` (`BWASK_KEY`, `ACT.bwAskNo`). `acctBar` waits for a first session after Continue without an account. Small numbers use the body font.
- **Your gym (r33, item 207):** `settings.gym` (absent unless set; `gymClean` in `normalize`): `u` (the unit the gym is labelled in, `gymUnit`, chosen in the section, `ACT.gymUnit` clears typed weights with Undo), `db` {lo, hi, st, fine, fineTo} (`dbListOf`, more than 300 weights refused), `kb`, `plates` (absent when all are on), `stack`. `gymRack(ex,plan)` is the list for a dumbbell or kettlebell lift logged per bell with no step of its own; `incOf(ex,plan,at)` reads it (`rackGap`), then `rackStep` (r32), then `gymStep` (two of the smallest plate for a bar or a two-sleeve machine, one for a landmine or a per-side lift, the stack step). `progStep(...,at)`, `lighterLoad`, `kinStart`, `capOf(ex,plan,top)` (the rack's top is no cap for a lift logged above it) and `atCapOf`, and `rackSnap` (from `snapUnit`) keep suggestions on the rack; `rackTol` is half the last shown digit, so a bell typed as shown in the other unit is that bell. IDX's `capped` uses `capOf`. `gymRefresh` re-reads a session in progress. `plateList` uses the plates on hand in their unit. `incSave` keeps a lift on the rack unless a number is typed (`m.typed`).
- **Effort scale (r33, item 206):** `settings.rirScale` ('half', 'rpe', absent for whole RIR). `rirScale`, `effWord`, `effShow` (RIR to what is shown), `effOpts`, `rirStripHTML` (a 12-column grid for half and RPE), `setStr`; `parseSets(...,rpe)` reads @ and a trailing "rpe" as RPE. What is stored is always RIR.
- **First page tour and sign out (r33, V; tour tightened in r34):** `obTourSlides` (five slides, titles only since r34, each a small picture in `.obv*` classes, aria-hidden, sample numbers in the unit chosen), `obTourHTML`, `mountObTour` (scroll-snap, dots `ACT.obTourPick`, every 4 s since r37 (was 5.5) until touched, never with reduced motion or in a test browser unless `IRONLOG_TOUR_AUTO`), `obTour`. Never reuse a class name the app has (`.tv` is the rest timer's and At a glance's). `ACT.acctSignOut`: Sign out removes the phone's copy once synced, the quiet `alt` link keeps it (`confirmBox` alt with `quiet:true`); either way `KEY+'.signedOut'` (`OB_OUT`) puts the app on the first page (`obGate` in `obActive`), except with a session in progress kept on the phone. Only a sign-in made in the app clears it (`obOutClear`). `cloudInit`'s finally re-renders the first page once the cloud check ends.
- **Adjusting the next sets (r34, V):** `assessSet(bi,si)` after a set is ticked, unticked or changed (`afterSet`, from `setDone` and the reps, load and RIR handlers) sets `b.adj={at,w,from,r,up,why}`: up on 2+ reserve over target with reps in range or 2+ past the top (3+ until r45; V: 14 on a 10 to 12 range left the load alone; now the same 2 as between sessions), down on 2+ under both the range and the session's expectation (`lastRFor` or the load target's minimum) when not stopped early; the load from `fitE` at mid-range (up, `jumpCap`) or bottom (down) at target RIR plus one, through `heavierLoad`/`lighterLoad` (rack-aware). `greyW`, `greyR` and `appLoad`/`rowBlank` read `b.adj`; `adjRows` rewrites filled-in rows still on the app's number and puts them back; `adjLine` draws the reason with Keep (`ACT.adjKeep`, `b.adjOff`). `settings.autoAdj===false` turns it off (`adjOn`). Draft blocks keep `adj` and `adjOff` in `normalize`.
- **Empty before a log (r34, V):** `noLog()`, `NIL` ('–'), `nz(v)`: the ring, At a glance, Stats tiles and subtitles, History, This week and the muscle map legend show a dash until there is a session.
- **Weigh-ins (r34, V):** `#obBw` on the Set up page saves today's weigh-in; `bwDueCard` on Today when the last weigh-in is `bwEvery()` days old (`settings.bwEvery`: 7, 30 or 0; absent 14; `BW_EVERY`), Later per device (`BWSNOOZE_KEY`). Bodyweight lifts are read at the latest weigh-in in IDX's `pts` (`load` is `prLoad` for them) and in the engine (`bwRefLoad`); `tonnage` keeps each day's.
