- **Freestyle mode (r34, V):** `settings.todayStart==='free'` (`freeMode()`): Today's card is a blank session (`startFree`) with the plan's day as a button; `todayRest` is everything below either card; `weekNeed` is 1, This week counts sessions and hard sets, adherence is not measured.
- **Two-a-days (r34):** sessions keep `t` (start time) and `sessCmp` orders them (date, `t`, id) in `sortedSessions`, `bySess` (sync chunks), `cycleState`, History; `weekSessions` counts sessions; `calDay` opens the `daypick` sheet for a day with two or more.
- **Rest weeks (r34, V):** `settings.restWeeks` (week starts), `isRestWeek`; `avgWeeks` reaches past them, `weekStreak` and adherence skip them, `recentLighter` and `coach` read them; `restwk` sheet, `ACT.restWk` with Undo.
- **Less text (r34, V, item 231):** a screen shows the number and the action; reasons go in a `tipi` or a How this works. `coach()` returns one line in `text` and the reason in `why`; `exHeadline` is one line with the change, rate, ranges and sessions behind an i. `fmtE` shows estimates (1RM, PR toasts, the e1RM chart and table, the bests board) in whole pounds or half kilos; loads keep `fmtW`. Strength return on volume says nothing on a rising row (one line below says keep the sets); Size check says a shared stretch once.
- **Licenses (r34, item 233):** `scripts/build.js` writes `LICENSES.txt` (each copied library's and font's license in full; the build fails if one is missing); Settings > Diagnostics links it when `window.IRONLOG_APP`.
- **Recently deleted (r34.1, V):** `trashDel` (one item, Undo) and `trashEmpty` (asks first) delete for good. A ready-made routine removed unchanged (`plainTemplate`, by `routineSig` against `tplSigs`) never goes there; `trashTidy` at start and the end of `cloudPull` clear ones earlier builds left. The Today card says Day N, not Day N of M.
- **The independent review of r34.1 (r34.2):** `BACK_DAYS` (28) and `daysOff(exId,opts)`: `suggest` reads a lift more than 4 weeks off as back (the lighter path, word "Back after N weeks", 2+ RIR, usual sets); IDX's `fresh` uses the same 4 weeks, and a lift that is not fresh has no trend word or "PR last time" (and, before r41 removed it, no projection) (`exHeadline` says when it was last done); `calDueIdx` skips it. `loadUp` in exStats: a heavier working load with the reps in range is never falling. `bwNoLoad(ex,b)` and `noAdd` in `suggestCore`: a bodyweight lift never loaded progresses by reps and is never adjusted mid-session. Every set at 5+ RIR steps at least once (`suggestCore`, `assessSet`). `rackTol(L,at)`: a typed load is the nearest bell within 45% of the gap. `barKg()` and `plateText` follow the gym's unit. `prName`/`prOverall` name a PR; prs carry `sid`, `prevAll`, `bb`; `big` is stronger than ever only. `adjSrc`/`adjSync` keep an adjustment on its own set. `hintOnce`/`HINT_ONCE` say a repeated hint once per session screen. `leftMin(d)` is 0 once every set is done (`min5` never shows 0). `effTgt(rir)` writes the plan's effort in the lifter's scale. `sessTm` gives History a start time when a day has two sessions. `overSets(n,plan,kind)` says "your first" only when the plan has more sets. Signed out with accounts, Your data has one line (`acctPanel`, no `#syncInfo`). `backupDue` is false while an account synced this week. Weight lifted skips only timed holds. The same lift twice in a session (V): `joinable`/`joinRows` join a swap or an add that lands next to the same exercise (with an `inv` Undo), `joinAdjacent` saves side-by-side blocks as one at Finish, and `sameExOnce` makes IDX read every block of one lift in a session as one exposure (blocks apart stay as logged). No other app is named on screen (the import parsers still read Strong and Hevy files). tests/r34b.js scans all copy for wording V ruled out (shared phones, someone else, another person) and em dashes, comments included (since r49 in the source files, as the page's bundled script keeps only some of them).
- **r35 (V's clean-up):** the Lifts section has one change number, the headline's (since r41 in loads, with `pct` and the slope behind its i, and no change tile). The Today card is Start plus `changeBtn` (Change) and More options; `startAltSheet` (kind `startAlt`, `ACT.startAltOpen`) holds Lighter day, Short on time, Blank session, Pick for me and the plan's days (`ACT.dayPick` sets `ui.todayDay`); its starts run through `ACT.altGo` (closes the sheet, then the card's old action). The grey-or-filled card (`loadAskCard`) is gone; `ACT.loadAsk` stays for the Settings switch. Settings' Demo data, Reset and Diagnostics are one group `more`. `assessSet` answers an easy set (2+ over the target RIR, reps under the top) with no heavier load that fits by the same load for more reps (`adj.w===adj.from`, said as reps by `adjLine`). `routineUpdates` adds `ss` (exercises done linked that the day has, not linked there) and `applyUpd` puts them side by side under one link.
- **r36 (V: the code optimization, proven to change nothing):** `IDX()` is six steps: `idxSessions` (weekly and daily counts, one exposure per lift per session), `idxAutoLighter`, `idxLightWeeks`, `idxPRs` (a lift's PRs and progress marks), `idxLift` (its trend, stall, signals, bests) and `idxScores`. `suggestCore` sets up one context `C` and tries its cases in order: `sgEasy` (top sets 2+ over the target reserve), `sgPastRange`, `sgBelowRange`, `sgNoStep` (heaviest available, fixed load, bodyweight never loaded), `sgTopOfRange`, `sgBadDay`, `sgReps`; `sgFirst` is no history. The code moved unchanged; `tests/r36.js` holds the numbers to r35's.
- **Lift goals (r38):** `ex.goal` {w, r} (absent unless set; `normalize` drops one on a timed or assisted lift). `goalState(exId)` gives `hit` (the first session with a working set at w or more for r or more), `cur` (best `fitE` of the last 84 days, deload and lighter left out), `need` (`fitE` of the goal at 0 RIR, bodyweight from `bwRefLoad` added), `pct` (no `weeks` since r41); `noBw` on a bodyweight lift with no weigh-in (the line asks for one, no percentage). A phone still on r37 or older rebuilds exercises from a fixed field list and drops `goal` when it syncs; update every device. `goalLine` under the lift's headline on Stats > Lifts; the `goal` sheet, `ACT.goalOpen`, `goalSave`, `goalClear`.
- **An exercise's muscles on the body (r39):** `exMusParts`, `exBodyFlat` (flat figures, `.mx` with `ex-main` or `ex-help`), `exMusWords`, `exMusLegend`; the `exMus` sheet (`ACT.bMus` from a session exercise's ⋯ menu) and the editor's `.exbody` (behind Show on body since r40, `ui.exBody`, `ACT.exBodyTog`). The 3D body is shared: `b3Host()` is the sheet's `#bm3dx` while it is open, else Today's `#bm3d`; `B3.ex` (main, help) colours it from `--volt` (`map3dColors().ex`), helpers lerped half to the body colour; `renderModal` mounts it in the sheet and `closeModal` hands it back to Today. A tap in the sheet names the muscle (a toast).
- **RIR 4 to 5 as half a set (r40):** `settings.rirHalf` (absent unless on; `rirHalfOn()` is also false with RIR off). `hardCredit(s,ex)` is a set's weekly credit (hard: `setCredit`; any working set past RIR 3 with the setting: half of it, with no upper bound, since Corrected RIR can read a call of 4 as 6), `halfLbl()` names the range in the effort scale in use (RIR 4 to 5, RIR 3.5 to 5, RPE 5 to 6.5) and `planCredit(it)` a planned item's; IDX's `weekSets`, `weekConf` and `weekHard`, `weekStats().got`, the recap's hard sets, `plannedSets` and `planSetsPerWeek` read them. `dayMus` and `dayMeta` (recovery, Pick for me) and `sessionMuscles` (the per-workout flag) keep hard sets only.
- **Numbers to act on (r41, V):** `trendScope(exps,anchor)` (full sessions, current range, the 6 weeks to the lift's own last session) and `trendW(sc)` (those with a reading; separate because `idxLift` sets `tv` after the scope is known) are the trend's sessions, and `trendEnds(w)` its two ends (better of two, or single with three), shared by `idxLift` and the screens. `liftChange(exId,dir,ow)` says the change in the estimated 1RM (`best`) of the two end sessions where `estInLoad(ex)` (compound, not bodyweight, an estimate, total load) and both have one, else the top set at each end (`topSetPt`, scored by reps alone), and shows a number only when it points the same way as the word (from an independent review of r41); `exHeadline` keeps the change and rate behind its i. No projection anywhere (`exProjection` removed). `nextLine` on Stats > Lifts is the `suggest` target for the plan of the next day due that has the lift (`nextDayWith`, Today's own starting day). `goalState` has no `weeks`; `goalLine` adds `goalGap` (the goal's load less `e1inv` of the best estimate at the goal's reps) on `estInLoad` lifts. When the Volume line carries the coach's words it still says Above. `dashSummary`: Moving by name, and when the coach's action points at a muscle in Volume (`act.sec==='volume'`) that the Volume line names, that line carries the coach's text and Change is left out; the Stats tiles were adherence, hard sets this week and the session length (since r45: sessions and PRs in the last 4 weeks, and the session length). Muscles' Strength return lists the muscles to act on and one line for those rising at target; `musTrend` is in words. `bwRate` carries `wk` and `bwWeekTxt` says it in the lifter's unit. Readiness shows once a check-in exists. Settings' Help has one feedback button (the sheet asks the kind).
- **Estimated minutes (r42, V):** `estMin(n)` (beside `min5`, in `src/app/format.ts` since r51) is the one place an estimated duration is worded ("~75 min"); callers round first as before (`min5`, `minTxt`, `leftMin`). Today, Plan's days, the session header, Stats' planned length, the routine picker, Short on time, Pick for me, the trim toast and the recap all call it. tests/hygiene.js fails on the wording typed anywhere else.
- **Your pace (r43, V):** `pace()` (`paceIn` in `src/app/time.ts` since r52, cached as the model's `_pace`, cleared by `invalidate`) is the median of `durMin` over `sessModelMin(ss)` (the time model for the sets a session logged, at its own ranges and the plan's rest) for the newest `PACE_N` (8) timed sessions in `PACE_DAYS` (84), the model reading the day's superset links and the session's time less cardio done before or after lifting on a day with one session (both from the r43 review), counting only ratios from `PACE_LO` to `PACE_HI` (a third to three); under `PACE_MIN` (3) it is 1. `dayMinutes(day,half,raw)` multiplies by it unless `raw`; so do `remainMin`, `shortPlan`'s cost and Pick for me's minutes, so every estimate agrees. Stats' Average session tile drops its planned figure while `pace()` is not 1. Conventions, said in the Guide's Today topic.
- **Your training (r44, item 271, V's answers 2026-10-13):** `settings.profile` {goal, exp, days, focus} (absent unless set; `profClean` in `normalize`; its choices `PROF_GOALS`, `PROF_EXP`, `PROF_DAYS`, `FOCUS_MAX`, `FOCUS_UP`, `MAINT_SHARE` live in `src/app/profile.ts` since r51, and in the data part before that, because `normalize` ran while the save loaded, before later parts' constants existed). `profRule(ex,p)` is the one rule set (heavy compounds, `profHeavy`: barbell, Smith and machine compounds with an estimate, less the hip and back accessories in `PROF_NOT_HEAVY`, for Strength or Both; everything for under a year; nothing for Size, isolation past a year or timed), read by `defPlan` (so new items, swaps out of the routine and freestyle lifts start there; `planFor(...,plain)` and `defPlan(id,plain)` skip it, and the pace model reads logged sessions that way) and `profApplyList` (Apply, `ACT.profApply`, only fields that differ, rest only raised and a rest of 0 left alone, under a year only reps in reserve raised, `syncDraftPlan` for exercises not started, Undo; `profApplyWhat` words it). `defBand(m,p)` is a muscle's default target with the focus; `appBand(m,p)` is the app's own target: the profile's default, brought down to the floor a first-run pick recorded in `settings.bandsFit` ({muscle: whole sets}, absent unless set; written only by `fitTargets`, cleared by Default targets, `ACT.bandsDefault`), never on a focus muscle. `profMoveBands(old,new)` moves a target only when it reads `appBand(m,old)` exactly; anything else was typed and stays, and a target typed back to the app's number is the app's again. Nothing is inferred (the r44 review found four bugs in inferring which targets were typed or fitted). Targets fitted before r44 had no floors: once, at start, on the device's own state (not in `normalize`, which also runs in the sync comparison and Undo), `fitRecordOld` records them when every target is exactly a first-run fit of the routine in use, the starter or the old default; any other mix counts as typed (the safe side), and a focus on a typed target says so in its toast. `fitTargets` records a focus muscle's floor too, unused while it is a focus, and keeps r29's guard (a second fit to another routine is refused). Default targets resets everything, and its Undo is an `inv` like `profSet`'s. The moved values are stored as copies, since Weekly set targets edits the arrays in place. `ACT.profSet`'s Undo is an `inv` that puts back the profile and each target it moved that still reads what it was moved to. `fitTargets` starts from `defBand`; `planEvidenceNote` names only targets below the profile's default too. `strengthPick` adds the strength focus to `rankExercises`; focus muscles count as priorities there. Settings' `profile` group (`profSettings`, first, with the time budget field moved in) and, on a first run, the `#obProf` fold at the top of the routine picker opened from Set up (a button, `ACT.obProfTog`; `profFields` drawn only while `ui.obProf` is on, so a re-render keeps it open; not on the Set up page itself, whose text r21 keeps under 400 characters). The routine picker marks templates within half a day a week of `days`. With no profile or Size every number is r43's (tests/r44.js).
- **The Last column (r44, V):** `lastOf` returns `prev`, the shown session's sets at its working load (`workLoad`/`atLoad`, the rule the grey reps follow, so row n of Last is the set row n repeats), formatted as in the Last line, and `prevOff` when a top single or a pyramid's other loads were left out (then the line above keeps every set); every path out of `suggestCore`, `suggestTimed` and the `suggest` wrapper carries it (lighter day, deload, back after a break), blocks store it (`newBlock`, `refreshBlock`, `gymRefresh`; `normalize` keeps strings only, 40 characters, 30 at most; absent in drafts from before r44) and Finish never saves it. `lastCell(b,i)` is set i of last time beside working row i (warm-ups and drops blank); `lastHead(b)` shortens the line above the sets to the session's name and date when the column shows the sets. The set number is the warm-up and drop button (`button.n`, `data-act="sWarm"`, 44 px touch); the W/D column is gone, so the inputs keep r43's width within 1 px at 320 px. tests/r36.js leaves `prev` and `prevOff` out of the suggestion comparison only. The feedback sheet writes its screenshot only when it matches `FB_SHOT_RE` (r44 security review).
- **Suggest for the library (r34):** `ACT.exSuggest` opens a Suggestion report with a custom exercise's details; library exercises never come from users without a build.
- **Settings merge (r33):** in `mergeChunk`'s core settings, a key absent from the base hashes as absent, so a setting one side added or Undo brought back is a change, not a conflict lost to the other side's absence.
- **Why this target (r47, V 2026-10-15):** `whyTarget(b)` (in `src/app/session.ts` since r53, beside `targetText`) is the reason behind a suggestion in the engine's own words: `b.hint` (a suggestion's `text`; a block renames it) less the `b.last` prefix the screen already shows on its own line, plus the `basis` note when the target came from an earlier full session (since r53 it says a session where the lift was trimmed or done lighter than usual does not set one, nor, on a lighter day, in a deload, or on an exercise made lighter, one with fewer reps or sets than usual; it said lighter and deload sessions as a whole). Nothing is recomputed, so there is no second account of the rules to drift from the first. It sits behind a `tipi` named "Why this target" beside the target on the session screen and beside Stats' `nextLine`, as V's less-text ruling asks (r34, item 231). Before r47 the engine's sentence showed only when there was no target at all, so on r46 none of ten targets on a demo session was explained. `tests/r47.js` and a check in `tests/coherence.js` keep the shown reason word for word the engine's.
- **What a lifter asks while training (r47, V 2026-10-15):** `bestLine(exId)` (beside `allTimeBests`) is the lift's heaviest set in the words `bestsBoard` uses, shown under the Last line on the session screen; it drops the date when that set was the lift's last session, since the Last line already carries it. Before this a lift's best was two taps away, under the exercise's menu. The Guide's How targets are set now says a target is not a record: a target is what to beat today, a record is the best ever done, a PR is the moment one is set. With nothing logged, Today points at History's Log a past workout (`#preApp`), which is how lifts done before the app become real bests: they are ordinary sessions, so they set bests, show under Stats, and the engine starts from them (reading a long gap as a layoff). `tests/bests.js`; before it, none of ten lifts showed its heaviest.
- **One index of sessions by date (r47, V 2026-10-15):** `dateIndex()` (`dateIndexIn` in `src/app/analytics.ts` since r52) is built once per change and holds `byDate` (each date's sessions in `sessCmp` order) and `weekN` (sessions per week); `sessionsOn(d)` reads it. The week bar, the calendar (`calBody`, `calDay`), the day picker, the past sheet, This month's title and Consistency's heatmap all read it, and a day with two sessions on the week bar goes through `calDay`, so it asks which as the calendar does; until r47 each grouped the log itself, and the week bar ordered a two-a-day by id where the day picker ordered it by start time, so the morning session could be named second. `weekStats().got` reads `IDX().weekHard` (the table the recap, Volume and the muscle views count from) instead of walking the log with its own copy of the rule. The index is a cache of its own (`_dix`, cleared by `invalidate` with IDX's), so IDX's object keeps r35's shape for tests/r36.js. The remaining readers that walk `state.sessions` themselves are lookups and rules (`recentLighter`, `readiness`, `sessionLength`, `calDueIdx`, `coach`'s empty-log wording, the editors' "has logged sets" checks), each on a quantity shown in one place; a quantity shown in two places is a function in IDX or `dateIndex`, and tests/coherence.js compares the places. A crash reaches the owner in one tap: the recovery screen offers Report this (`data-crash`), which opens the problem report written (the screen and the error) with `crash:true` in the context, and `log` keeps the first lines of the stack (`s`) with each error so the report says where it broke. `logCut` takes a URL's query and fragment out of every kept message and stack (the page's URL holds a reset link's token in its fragment for a moment), keeping each frame's file and line. `fbContext` guards every read of the log, so a report about a crash survives the crash, and drops its oldest errors until it is under `FB_CTX_MAX` (15,000 bytes), since the feedback table refuses a context over 20,000 and with it the whole report.
- **Speed as the log grows (r47):** `tests/perf.js` times ticking a set, the Today and Stats screens, and an analytics rebuild at three years of training (624 sessions, about 11,000 sets), against ceilings about four times what was measured on a quiet desktop (tick 21 ms, Stats 70 ms, rebuild 91 ms), so a change several times worse fails and ordinary variation does not. Chromium only (`WEBKIT_SKIP`): one engine's milliseconds on a shared runner say nothing about another's.
- **The documents against the repository (r46):** `tests/hygiene.js` checks that every repo file the documents name exists, that nothing they record as removed is back in the source, and that CLAUDE.md neither calls the retired artifact a shipping channel nor names a build it is not. CLAUDE.md described that artifact as a live shipping channel for 17 days after `OWNER.md` recorded it retired, and 147 lines of its code stayed in `src/` because of it.
- **r53 (V 2026-10-18: a day's label never overrides what a lift did; a PR is a PR whenever it happens; the audit's fixes, then the independent review's):** `idxTaggedFull` (step 2b of `IDXIn`, after the lighter readings) clears deload and lighter from a lift's exposure when `atUsualLevel` says it was done at its usual level against its previous full sessions in the range, up to three: as many sets at its working load as each of theirs (a lighter day halves the sets, and two sets of 10 after four sets of 10, 10, 9 and 8 read as usual per set and made the next target add load off half the work, the review found; against the fewest of theirs, one session stopped at half let it through again, the second review found); then, with a strength reading on it and on one of them, `atUsualLoad` (the r30 audit's test: the heaviest load no lighter, the reading within `USUAL_SHARE`, 97%); without one (a timed hold, a lift in `NO_E1`, a bodyweight lift before any weigh-in, sets past 20 reps), the working load no lighter than any of theirs and the reps a set within 97% of each (a convention of r53's, stricter than on the reading: 12, 12 and 11 after 12s is within it, 12 and 11 is not); with nothing before it, the label stands. A trimmed exercise stays trimmed. Such a lift sets its targets, trend, stalls and Last line; the rest of the day stays lighter. Not yet: a promoted session is never one of the sessions step 2 compares later ones with, since it runs first, and `planForIn` reads the day's label (PENDING 313). `idxPRsIn` records PRs on every day, and only `big` ones are counted and shown; on a lighter day or in a deload the PR shows at Finish, not as the set is logged (`refreshPR`, legacy, PENDING 311). `planForIn`: a lift on no day of the routine plans from its own last full session (not sample, deload, lighter, trimmed or made lighter): its working sets and, where `profRuleIn` sets no range, its range; its reps in reserve stay the plan's or the profile's, since last time's as the target would hide a load that was too light (`sgEasy`); never logged, `defPlanIn` as before. `routineUpdatesIn` (moved from the legacy script to change): Finish's offer on a lighter day or in a deload adds each exercise at `planForIn`'s sets for that day of that routine, not the halved ones. `recentLighterIn`: the coach holds a deload back, within 4 weeks, after a lighter week read from what the lifts did, not the labels: a session is lighter when half or more of its lifts were done lighter (still deload, lighter or read lighter after step 2b), and a week when more than half its sessions were, once it has ended or, while under way, from the second, or, for a deload begun mid-week, when its last two or more sessions were deloads done lighter, a deload session being one tagged deload or any in the week marked as one, since a blank or Freestyle session there is not tagged (PENDING 311; the sixth review) (the coach's own Deload week button marks the week from the day it is tapped, so a lifter on four days who taps it on Wednesday deloads two of four, the fourth review; only deloads make that run, since two Lighter days on Thursday and Friday after three normal days held the deload back where the same two on Monday and Tuesday did not, and a heavy and light programme with its light days late in the week never had one suggested, the fifth review); and after a rest week, or a week marked as a deload with nothing trained in it (`deloadWeek`). A deload begun mid-week (deloads Wednesday and Friday after a normal Monday) is a lighter week, the first review's case; one Lighter day or one deload session in a week otherwise trained is not, nor a deload done at full for every lift, nor the mark alone on a week with training in it, whose sessions decide (the second and third reviews). The weekly volume average still leaves out only weeks where every session was labelled deload or lighter, or half or more of the lifts trained read lighter, two at least (`lightWk`, r30), and Stats' last lighter week line reads a third way (`lastLighterWeek`, legacy); one reading of a lighter week for all three is PENDING 312. `idxLiftIn`: no stall while an injury the lift loads is flagged (`exInjuryIn`); the sessions held for it are not recorded, so once the injury is cleared the lift can read stalled until its next session (PENDING 311). A PR in the lift's range on a session left out of its stall's stretch (trimmed or done lighter) since the third-last session of the stretch is movement, as one in the stretch is (`offSince`; on a lift with a reading only a PR Recent PRs lists, stronger than ever, since 90 x 9 on a Lighter day after 100 x 8 is no sign a plateau ended and took a needed deload away again, the sixth review, so `idxLiftIn` is given the PR list; on a lift with none, any PR; the fifth review: a lift that set a PR on yesterday's Lighter day read stalled beside it in Recent PRs, and r52 did the same after a trimmed session); `tests/coherence.js` now holds Stalls against Recent PRs as a lifter reads them, and fails on r52. The Guide (`guide.ts`) states each. `tests/r53.js` runs the app's own flows where one exists (logs written directly for the rest) and fails on r52, and on r53's own earlier versions where the review or the checks found them wrong (8 leg raises on a lighter day counted in full and made the next target 17+ reps instead of 25+; half the sets added load, also with one earlier session stopped at half; a mid-week deload; a deload week marked after a normal week; deload sessions done at full; two Lighter days under way; one deload session late in a normal week; a four-day lifter's deload tapped on Wednesday; two Lighter days late in a week holding the deload back; a lift read stalled the day after a PR on a Lighter day; a Lighter day's 90 x 9 ending a stall; a Freestyle lifter's deload begun mid-week not counted); `tests/r53w.js` compares r52 and r53 on the wide histories call by call, injuries and PRs on days left out of a stall included (`prOff` in `r53Touches`), each difference held to the rule that makes it, the calls given a lift's own plan asked again in r52, and every PR checked against r52's on the same log with the labels taken off, then past midnight (its suggestions on a lift's own plan asked of r52 at each read), stale caches and the demo; `tests/r36.js` still compares r35, its tagged lifts made lighter than their usual (`honestTags`) and the coach compared as r53 changes it (`coachSame`, both in `tests/wide.js`), and where a PR on a day left out of a stall lifts one r35 read, that stall, the muscle scores it set and the coach compared as `tests/r53w.js` compares them (`freed`, `idxSame`, `coachOk`, shared in `tests/wide.js`). `tests/moved.js` gained `edits` (code changed after its move), a check that an edit takes away no name it does not state (a new local that shadows one), Function, Reflect, Proxy and WebAssembly off the globals new code may read, and a normalizer that renames from the code as esbuild reprints it, since the minifier picks names by character counts and a comment or an escape gave the same code two names; `tests/hygiene.js` refuses `var` in `src/app/`, since a nested one would change a name's meaning past that reading.
- **Test hooks:** `window.__ironlog` exposes state and most functions, and it is how the tests reach in. Keep it. Since r52 it reaches all 74 names `live.ts` gives the legacy script (22 were added at its end, which `tests/moved.js` reads back and `tests/r52.js` adds to r51's page for the comparison), 75 since r53.
- **WebKit (2026-10-14):** every browser on an iPhone is WebKit, and V uses one, so every suite also runs in Playwright's WebKit (`npm run test:webkit`, four shards on GitHub, and the `test` check needs them). A suite left out of that run is in `WEBKIT_SKIP` in `tests/run.js` with the reason (the four that open no browser, hygiene, moved, owner-doc and sql; perf, whose timings hold for Chromium only; and, since r52, engine, which compares the engine in Node with Chromium's answers while `tests/r52.js` and `tests/r53w.js` compare the builds in WebKit). What the harness does for WebKit (`tests/h.js`): a page opened from a file is served its own files with an `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` header, since WebKit refuses a file page its fonts (origin null) where the installed app, on one https origin, never meets this; object and data URLs pass through; `touchDriver(page)` is the finger for drags, real touches in Chromium and in WebKit touches built with `document.createTouch` as iOS has them, which SortableJS takes on its Safari path (touch events, never pointer events). WebKit moves a tap to a button or handle near the finger, as iPhones do, so a check that taps blank space picks a point with nothing tappable within 30 px (tests/essentials.js). Playwright's WebKit is the engine on Linux, not an iPhone: the user agent is desktop Safari's, there is no home-screen mode, no on-screen keyboard and no iOS storage eviction, and a drag is built events rather than a finger. Those stay V's to check on the phone (`OWNER.md`, Checks on your phone).
- **Editing (r36):** change `src/`, run `npm run join`, then the tests. `npm run join:check` says whether `index.html` matches `src/`.

### Rules for changes

1. Don't break what works. Run `npm test` before and after. Add or extend a suite for every behaviour you change.
2. A number shown in two places is one function, and the screens are checked against each other by `tests/coherence.js`. Extend it whenever a release adds or moves a number a lifter reads. Passing suites say behaviour did not change, never that it is right: three PR counts, two meanings of a week's sessions, and a rest week with a plan target all shipped from r38 to r45 with every suite green, and that suite finds all of them in one run.
3. Never lose data. Any change to stored shape needs a `normalize()` default, and a migration if it changes meaning, plus a case in `tests/migration.js`.
4. The math follows the evidence review summarised under "Evidence base" below. Don't change a number without a source. Label conventions as conventions, in a tip.
5. Copy is short, plain and specific. No em dashes, no hype, no exclamation marks. The default screen shows the number and the action; the reasoning goes in a `data-tip` or a `?` help toggle.
6. Phone first: 44 px touch targets, no horizontal overflow at 320 px, and light and dark both checked. `tests/charts-layout.js` checks overflow.
7. Code comments explain why, not what, in full sentences. That is the house style of this file.
8. Bump `APP_VERSION` in `src/app/config.ts` for every published build, and `DISPLAY_VERSION` (what people see: 2.0 from r49, the minor number up one per release merged, the major for a large change) with it; `STATE_VERSION` only when the stored shape changes.
9. **No shortcuts, no workarounds, no exceptions (V, 2026-10-17).** The proper thing is done the first time: a cast where a type belongs, an export with no comment, a value typed in two places, a module exempted from the formatter, a check set aside, a rule remembered instead of tested, a document left out of date, are each a shortcut, and confessing one afterwards does not cut it. When the proper thing costs more, it is still what is done. A new rule gets a test that fails without it, and the list of such tests in `tests/hygiene.js` only grows. Nothing in `src/app/` is hand-coded where it can be derived from one source. Nothing that works for a lifter, on screen or in a flow, is weakened for the sake of the code. Report what was verified and how, and what was not checked; never a problem that does not exist.

### Publishing

- **Standalone app:** work on a branch and open a pull request; `.github/workflows/publish.yml` runs every suite on it, in Chromium and in WebKit, and, if they pass, publishes its preview build to `gh-pages` under `preview/` (https://ironapp.org/preview/; one slot, the latest pull request wins). V tries it on the phone. Merging it into `main` (V's approval) runs the suites again and, if they pass, publishes dist/ to the `gh-pages` branch (leaving `preview/` alone), which GitHub Pages serves at https://ironapp.org. By hand, if the workflow cannot run: `npm test`, then `git worktree add ../pages gh-pages`, copy dist/* in, commit, push. Open apps show "New version ready" the next time they come to the foreground.
- **When a check fails on GitHub:** the job log needs a GitHub sign-in that Claude's machine does not have, so `tests/run.js` also writes each failing line as an annotation, which the API serves (`/repos/vqx7/Ironlog/check-runs/<job id>/annotations`). Suites never reach outside services: sign-up suites answer the breach check with `quietHibp` (`tests/fake-supabase.js`); the first r26 run failed before that was added. A suite stopped at the 15-minute limit says so, with the last check it passed, even though Playwright catches the stop signal and exits with its own code (until 2026-10-14 such a stop said nothing). r33's stop on 2026-10-14 came after its last check, in teardown, and which step stalled was not recorded. The likeliest is the stand-in server's close: r33 left four devices open on it, and its close waits for any request still open (shown in Node with a half-sent request; not seen on GitHub). Whether the r43 and r44 stops had the same cause is not known. Now the devices are closed, the server's `close` drops its connections, and teardown goes through `settle` (`tests/h.js`), which lets go after 30 s with a `note` line naming the step; `tests/run.js` writes every `note` line of a passing suite as a notice on GitHub, so a stall that comes back says where (`/check-runs/<job id>/annotations`).
- **Claude artifact:** retired by V on 2026-09-27. Nothing is published there; do not republish it.
- After publishing, copy the build it replaced into `baselines/` and add it to `tests/migration.js`.
- Merging: since 2026-10-01 (`OWNER.md` D2 done) a merge on GitHub's website uses V's private noreply address, so V can merge there. When V instead says to publish, Claude makes the merge commit itself (author Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>, message "Merge pull request #N from vqx7/<branch>") and pushes it to `main`; since `main` is protected (`OWNER.md` B6, done 2026-10-15) only the website merge works. Four older merge commits (pull requests #1 to #4) carry V's Gmail address; V chose not to rewrite history (PENDING 84).

(The evidence base it ended with is in `docs/EVIDENCE.md`.)

## Since r54: the code map, release by release

Each release adds here the names it made or changed and why, so the map stays searchable without growing CLAUDE.md.

### r54 (2026-10-18, version 2.5; PENDING 315 and 316)

- **The audit's fixes (PENDING 315, V: every one).** Each case is in `tests/r54.js` and fails on r53.
- **Dumbbells with nothing logged:** `rackStepIn(M, ex, at)` reads the load in hand (`at`) as the rack's one known weight when the lift has no logged load (6 kg gives a 2 kg step, so 8 kg, not 8.5); `incOfIn` passes it. Not kept, since it changes with the load.
- **A log read in the other unit:** `snapUnitIn` counts a load as typed only when it is exact in the unit on screen (within 1e-6 of a 0.05 step), rounds down under a target of reps (`dn`), and restates "add X" and "take X of help off" for the step the shown load took.
- **Effort in the lifter's scale:** `effScale(v)` and `effAt(scale, rir)` (`format.ts`) are the one rule for the screens (`rirScale`, `effShow` in `effort.ts`, moved there) and the engine's sentences (`sgEasyIn`, `easierTextIn`); RPE is 10 minus RIR.
- **How long a lift has stalled:** `stallStretch(wE)` (`analytics.ts`) is the stretch the stall test reads (the last 6 sessions in the range, widened to 21 days, at least 4); `stallWeeksIn(M, id)` its span in weeks, null for a lift with no reading (its test reads the last 3 sessions), and `stallWordsIn(M, id)` the one wording the coach's top-of-range line and `exHeadline` say: over the last N weeks, over N weeks when sessions held for an injury came after the stretch, over its last 3 sessions (both from `stallSpans`, over `stallReads`, the sessions the test reads; from the independent review of r54, the span counted the held sessions and the coach and the headline each wrote the words); `STALL_WHY` (`coach.ts`) is the rule in words, the coach's tip and the headline's.
- **One reading of a lighter week:** `lighterTally` (a session is lighter when half or more of its lifts were done lighter; a week, by its sessions and its closing run of deload sessions done lighter) feeds `idxLightWeeks` (more than half the sessions, or a run of two or more), which the weekly averages leave out, `recentLighterIn` (the deload suggestion: those weeks, the week in progress from its second lighter session, rest weeks, a week with nothing trained, and a marked deload week with nothing trained while it runs) and `lastLighterWeekIn` (Stats' line under Stalls: completed weeks, rest, a week off or lighter, nothing until 4 weeks trained or just after two empty weeks). Its words: `LIGHTER_WEEK_WORDS`, `LIGHTER_WEEK_TIP`. Until r54 the averages read the labels (r30), Stats read hard sets against the usual week (65%), and the coach read what the lifts did (r53).
- **The log's first week:** `avgFirstWeekIn(M)` is the week after the one the log starts in; `avgWeeksIn` starts there (until r54, `firstFullWeekIn`, which adherence still reads). `FIRST_WEEK_REASON` and `FIRST_WEEK_WHY` say why (the week loads are found). `regionSrc` labels a cut average "the N full weeks after your first week", shows the first week alone as "your first week, to <date>" (`first`, not `old`), and its note says "No full week to average yet". The coach with no average: "after your second week" until the first counted week is over, "after a full week of training" after that, the break line as before.
- **The plan reaching the target:** `coachIn` says "Your plan has P: do every planned set" when `plannedSetsIn` (moved from the legacy `plannedSets`) already meets the muscle's minimum and the lifter is not in Freestyle; "Add N sets" only when the plan is short.
- **Every other day:** `plannedPerWeek(R)` takes a cycle's rate down to the whole number, at least one for a routine that trains (V: 3.5 is 3), for the ring, This week, the streak (`weekNeedIn`, `weekStreakIn`) and adherence.
- **Build my own routine:** `ACT.obOwn` makes Training, Rest, Training, Rest, Training, Rest, Rest.
- **Past workouts:** `pastDraft` never sets one up as a deload; the deload week's mark does not store the day it was tapped.
- **History:** a deload session's name drops its " (deload)" beside the deload tag.
- **A cycle's weeks (317 d and the review's (2)):** `plannedPerWeek` and the plan's sets a week follow the whole sessions a week counts as on plan (`dayShare`; Strength 5 x 5: 39, not 46); `cycleMixIn` (`weeks.ts`) finds a muscle under its minimum only by which days of a cycle fell in the weeks averaged, every session and set due done and the plan reaching the target, and `muscleStatusIn` (moved from the legacy script) reads it as on plan (`'plan'`), the one status the coach, Stats' summary, Muscles, Volume, the radar and the map read (`ON_PLAN_TIP`). `refitIn` (`profile.ts`) moves, once at start, targets a first-run pick fitted by the old reading to r54's fit, typed targets left alone.
- **No average yet:** `avgWaitIn(M)` (`weeks.ts`) is the one reason there is no weekly average (back from a break, the log's second week, or a full week to come), said alike by the coach, Stats' summary, Recent average and a muscle's headline.
- **lb dumbbells (317 g):** `rackGridIn` (`engine.ts`) reads lb dumbbells with Your gym not set on a 5 lb rack (2.5 lb where the weights are on it); a weight off it steps to the rack's next weight in every path that steps a load (`gridSnap` after `unitSnap` in `snapUnitIn`).
- **One rule for every session (312 E, 317 e and f):** `idxShort` (`analytics.ts`) reads each lift's session against its usual, the middle of it and the two before it in the range since its last break (the two alone for a labelled session), and marks it `short: 'sets'` (fewer than its usual sets, never more than the plan's) or `short: 'reading'` (a reading under `USUAL_SHARE`, 97%, of the usual; with no reading a lighter working load or fewer reps a set). Its sets are `setsAt(q, usualW)`: its working sets within a tenth of its working load either way (`LIGHTER_LOAD`) and any heavier one at its usual working load, and on a step up its sets from the old working load up too; `usualSets` the middle of the sessions it is read against. Its reading is `readingOf(q, k)` (reps alone) or `repsOf(q, k)` (reps a set) over its first k sets at the working load, k the usual sets, so a set past them never counts against it; `atUsualLoad` and `atUsualWork` read the same way. From the independent review of r54 (commit 2478d73): counting only the sets at the working load read a lighter or heavier set among them, and the sets after a load lowered mid-session, as fewer sets, and the reading over every set let an extra set hold back a target the planned sets beat, on a lighter day too. `lighter(x)` (deload, light or read lighter) and `offTarget(x)` (lighter or short) in `formulas.ts` are the one test: a lighter session sets no target or trend; a short one sets no target and counts for the trend, stalls and PRs. A labelled lift done in full keeps its label as `tag` with the flags cleared. `sessionWord`/`sessionWordsIn` name each lift's reading for the Last line and History ("fewer sets", "below usual", "lighter", "deload"). The trimmed mark (`cut`) is no longer written; `normalize` keeps one already saved, which nothing reads.
- **Breaks:** `afterBreak(prev, date)` (`formulas.ts`, more than `BACK_DAYS`, 28, between two sessions of a lift) restarts the lift's lighter reading, its usual, its trend (`trendScope`'s `from`) and its stall; `backDaysIn` (`engine.ts`, private) keeps the engine on its back-after-a-break path until a session after the break sets a target.
- **Stalls per range (312 A):** `stallScopesIn(M, id, sc)` gives the ranges a lift's stall is read in (its newest, and the routine's planned ranges with a session in the last 42 days); `idxLiftIn` reads it stalled only when every one is, and `stallWeeksIn` gives the shorter span. Sessions held for an injury (`held`, from the saved exercise's `inj`) are left out of the stall test and of the span it says (`stallReads`). `atRangeTopIn(M, id)` (`coach.ts`) is the one test of a stalled lift at the top of its range, for the coach and `exHeadline`: the last session not done lighter, every set at its working load at the top of the range the routine plans for the lift on the day that session was done on (found by the session's day, else by the range it was done at, else the lift's plan), as the plan is now; until r54 each read the lift's plan with no day, the first day's on a lift on two days, so a light day's sets over the heavy day's range read as due a step up.
- **The plan a lift is done at (313 a, a2, d):** `planForIn` moved to `src/app/plans.ts` (layer 2) with `routineUpdatesIn`: a lift on no day of the routine takes its last session that sets a target, as the analytics read it, up to the date logged; `basePlanIn` (`routines.ts`) is the routine's plan or the lift's default. Finish's offer after a lighter day or deload adds an exercise at its full count, kept on the block as `full` (313 g).
- **The injury hold (311 g):** Finish saves `inj` on a held exercise; `normalize` keeps a valid one (`KNOWN_SEX`, `records.ts`), absent unless held.
- **PRs as the set is logged (311 a):** `refreshPR` marks and announces a PR on any day; a bodyweight lift before any weigh-in has its rep PRs counted (`idxPRsIn`'s `big`, `livePR`).
- **A change of units (the 155 lb case of 315):** `typedUnit(w)` reads the unit a load was typed in from its number (a whole number of `TYPED_STEP`, 0.05, in kg or in lb, never both); `prSameLoad(w, earlier)` gives the earlier load typed in the other unit that a set stands for (above it by no more than half the largest of `LOAD_STEPS` its own number fits), else its own load. `idxPRsIn` judges each set at that load, earlier sets as logged, and shows the estimate as logged (`shown`) in its band (`bandJ`); `prFrameIn(M, exId, s, date)` gives the live check (`livePR`) and the toast (`prBeat`) the same sessions, bests, heaviest load and judged load.
- **One source for steps and plates:** `DEFAULT_STEP` (5 lb, 2.5 kg), `TYPED_STEP` and `onStep(v, step)` in `units.ts`, read by `unitStepIn`, `stepReadIn`, the engine's lighter-day help and typed-load test and `roundLoadIn`; `LOAD_STEPS` (`library.ts`, the dumbbell rack's spacings, `RACK.dumbbell`); `gymPlates` returns copies of `PLATES_KG` and `PLATES_LB`; `BAND_SHORT` merged into `BAND_WORDS`, which held the same words.
- **Assisted lifts (315 i):** `sgFirstIn` suggests no help for an assisted lift with nothing logged; `emptyLoad(ex)` (`loads.ts`) is an empty load field's grey text, 0 only on a bodyweight lift that is not assisted; Finish asks "Add the help you used?" for a set ticked without help.
- **Freestyle in a deload week (311 c):** `startFree` makes a deload session there, so an exercise added comes in at half its sets. **Goal (311 e):** `goalState` reads every set of the last 12 weeks.
- **The sign-in sheet's fields (311 f, 309 c):** `morphInto(el, html)` (`src/app/sheets.ts`, layer 4) redraws the account sheet in place when only attributes and words change (`renderModalBody(keep)`), its error line always present and hidden when empty, so the field being typed in is never replaced. `wakeTip` waits for a message showing to go before its one-time tip (it took a PR's place). No code follows the keyboard: `followViewport`, which kept sheets in the part of the screen that shows, was taken out after V's look at the preview (on iOS 26 Safari the page showed under the sheet, then moved as the keyboard came). A sheet you type in opens tall instead, its fields at the top: the style sheet reads it from the sheet's own fields (`.sheet:not(.fixed):has(...)`, phones and touch screens), so no list of sheets is kept (PENDING 322). `renderModal` adds the labels' i buttons (`tipLabels`) before it puts a sheet's scroll back, so a sheet scrolled to its bottom keeps its place when redrawn.
- **The 3D body (314):** `closeModal` stops a turn still running when the exercise's muscles sheet closes.
- **The way of working (PENDING 316):** `tests/moved.js` deleted; `tests/r53w.js` compares r52 with `baselines/r53.html`; `tests/engine.js` reads the adapters from `live.ts` and calls those the digest does not (`EXTRA`, with the routine helpers the legacy script reads from `routines.ts`) after the digest that the pinned comparisons share.

### r55 (2026-10-19, version 2.6; PENDING 323, 324 and 326 to 328)

- **V's answers of 2026-10-19 (323), and the journey audit of r54 (324).** Each case is in `tests/r55.js` or `tests/r55b.js` and fails on r54.
- **Missed sets (323 a):** `isMissed(s)` (`formulas.ts`) is reps typed as 0; `isWork` leaves such a set out, so it counts toward no hard set, PR, estimate or target, and `normalize` keeps its 0 (no change of shape). `missedTag` and `MISSED_SAID` (`format.ts`) mark it in History, the folded line, `setStr` and the Last line; exposures carry the missed sets in `miss` with their place (`MissedPoint`), and `lastOf` lists them in order and leaves them out of the Last column (`prevOff`). `greyW` skips them. `sDone` and auto-mark tick a typed 0 as missed; a done set whose reps are cleared is unticked.
- **A load far off (323 b):** `oddLoadIn(M, b, si, date)` (`engine.ts`, `ODD_SESSION` 3, `ODD_BEST` 2) says why a working set's load is far off; `setDone` asks Log it or Fix it before it is logged (Fix it puts the cursor in the load), for odd reps (`oddReps`) and odd loads alike, at a tick and at Finish's Mark it done. Since r60 (V on the r59 preview, 2026-10-25, PENDING 353) it also names a load under the empty bar of a lift on the full bar typed as the total (`underBarIn`, reading `loadFloorIn`, the bar in Settings or the exercise's own), on any set, a warm-up or a drop included, after the session's and the log's comparisons. `sAdd` runs `assessSet` on the last set done before + Set copies it.
- **Settings > More (323 c):** one demo button; Diagnostics shows the version until `logBuf` holds an error.
- **Falling in two ranges (323 d, PENDING 319):** `idxLiftIn` reads the trend of each range `stallScopesIn` gives (`trendOf`), and `falling` only when every range falls; `rrIn` names the range when there are two, `holdRR` the ranges not falling, and `exHeadline` says them with `FALL_WHY`.
- **Logging (324):** `rerenderKeepScroll` keeps the focused field's text when it reads as the stored number; an untick within 30 s clears the grey numbers it wrote and puts back auto-reorder's move (`ui.ordBack`); the reps field stores and shows whole reps; `blockProgress` counts reps past the top as the top and says "Fewer sets than usual: the target stays" with fewer sets; `applyUpd` gives a swapped-in lift its plan as done (`RoutineUpdate.swap[].plan`); `swapBlock` asks to move every logged set or add the lift; a lighter day's header says `effLeastShort(scale, LIGHT_RIR)`; Back keeps a sheet with typing (`DIRTY_SAID`); `render` stops the timer with no session; the first page offers Undo after Erase everything; the CSV export and `impFromTable` carry session notes, minutes, missed sets and cardio.
- **The sessions after (324):** `lastOf`'s grey reps come from the target's session when a short session was at another load; `allTimeBestsIn` (moved to `analytics.ts`) judges loads across units with `prSameLoad` and reads up to a date (a past workout's `bestLine`); the engine's `planPts` (the plan's sets), `noHelp` (an assisted lift at no help), the rep cap at `repMax`, a weighted bodyweight lift with no weigh-in stepped as a loaded one, `LIGHT_SHARE` and `LIGHT_RIR`, and a timed hold back after a break (`suggestTimedIn`).
- **Plan, the weeks and the screens (324):** `dayShare` in Plan's header; `weekBar` plans nothing in a rest week; `rNewDo` lays out rest days; `trainOn(d)` (`routines.ts`) makes a rest day given an exercise a training day, named so when it was named Rest; `metSt` (`volume.ts`) counts a 0 minimum met with nothing logged; `pwOf(routines, id)` (`routines.ts`) is what each new session records at Finish as `pw` (`cleanSess` keeps 1 to 7, absent otherwise, so older records keep their hash; an edit keeps the session's own through `keepExtra`), and `weekRoutineIn` keeps `weekPw`, which `weekNeedIn` reads before the routine as it is now (adherence reads `weekNeed` too); `logStartIn` and `logBackIn` (`weeks.ts`) start the weeks counted after a break of more than `BACK_DAYS`, read by `firstFullWeekIn` and `avgFirstWeekIn`, with `BACK_WEEK_WHY`, `avgWaitIn`'s `why` and the averages' `_back` wording it; `bestSet` breaks a tie by reps; `idxLiftIn`'s `trendOf` reads no trend for a bodyweight lift with no weigh-in (its readings would be the added load alone), and `exHeadline` asks for one; `effIn` and `effName` (`effort.ts`) put Plan's effort field in the lifter's scale; Strength return on volume says `musTrend`; `planItemOf` (`routines.ts`, the search `routinePlanIn` uses) lets `syncDraftPlan` reach a lift whose plan came from the item edited.
- **The independent review of r55 (326):** `savedEx` and `draftSession` (`session.ts`) are how Finish saves a block and how a first link keeps another device's workout in progress; `stripDemo` and `hasDemo` (`chunks.ts`) are the one way demo data leaves a log (Clear demo data, Finish, a first link, an empty account); a first link adopts the account's copy only when the phone holds no routine or custom exercise of its own either; `quietRR` (`idxLiftIn`) names a range with too few sessions to read; `tests/hygiene.js` fails on code hidden after a comment on a legacy line.
- **Data and accounts (324):** a first link adopts the cloud copy only when the phone holds nothing of its own (by content, never by what it held at start), merges without demo records and offers no Undo; `acctOpen` from a problem report goes back to it after sign-in (`exBack`); `dropStarter` (`routines.ts`) is the one way a first-run choice replaces the untouched starter, Build my own included; a first-run pick applies Your training as Apply does; the update bar sits at the top on the first-run pages; section summaries keep their longest word at 320 px; `.check-inline` rows and Plan's day names are 44 px.
- **The end-to-end audit of r55, data and accounts (327):** `cloudStop(forget)` (`src/14-account.js`) forgets the sync notes (`cloud.acct`, `cloud.meta`, `cloud.editAt`, `cloud.last`) only when the log or the account is gone (Delete account, Sign out and remove, Erase everything signed out); Sign out with Keep on this phone leaves them with the copy, so signing back in merges from them. `addMeas` adds a second tape save the same day to the day's entry, with Undo. A load field tapped right after typing reps sets `pendAuto`, and the set ticks when that field is left (the `focusout` handler); a ticked set's load changed to one far off goes through `setDone`'s question again. `impTextSets` reads a unit written on a pasted line for that line; the import preview's cardio distances use `distU()`.

- **The second review of r55 (328):** a log replaced while signed out (a backup restored, Copy my log in, demo data loaded, Erase everything) runs `cloudStop(true)` when `!cloud.on`, so the notes kept with the old copy are forgotten and the next sign-in is a first link. `draftSession` keeps a kept workout's check-in (`cleanChk`) and start (`t`); `draftCardio(d)` (`session.ts`) is the one reading of a workout's cardio rows, for Finish and for the first link's kept workout. `putById(list, items)` (`util.ts`) puts records in by id, replacing a copy under the same id: Finish puts its session and cardio in with it. `setDone(bi, si, val, again)`: `again` for a set the far-off question unticked, ticked again by Log it or by the load fixed after Fix it (`ui.refix`, `store.ts`), its rest not started over. `impTextSets` reads a unit glued to the x and # as pounds.

### r55.1 (2026-10-20, version 2.7; PENDING 325 a, 327, 328 d and 330)

- **A light 0 (325 a):** `lightZeroIn(M, b, si, date)` (`engine.ts`, beside `oddLoadIn`) is a ticked 0 at a load under `LIGHTER_LOAD` of the heavier of `b.sw` and today's heaviest working set, loads with the bodyweight share; `setDone` asks Log it as missed or Fix it for it, a ticked set's reps changed to 0 go through `setDone` again, and Finish's Mark it done reads it.
- **Imports:** `IMP_COLS` reads `snote` (Workout Notes, description), `dur` (Strong's Duration, only with Strong's own columns), `end` (Hevy's end_time) and `ss` (superset_id); `impSpan(dur, start, end)` is the minutes; `impBuild` returns `ssOf` (each session's supersets by lift) and `routineFromLog(sessions, ssOf)` groups the day's items by its latest session's.
- **A deleted account:** `scripts/cloud-supabase.js`'s `friendly` turns 23503 and `user_not_found` into the code `gone`; `cloudFail` signs out, forgets the notes and says so. `tests/fake-supabase.js` keeps a deleted account's other tokens valid, its writes refused with 23503 and its user with user_not_found.
- **Quick entry:** `fillSets` ticks each set not far off in order and hands the first far one to `setDone`'s question.
- **Units:** `prSameLoad` allows half the shown digit (`TYPED_STEP` in the typed unit); `blockProgress` judges a heavier set at that load.
- **Plates:** `plateGridIn(M, ex, plan)` (`engine.ts`) is a bar lift's grid (bar, pair of the smallest plate) when Your gym's plates allow one; `plateSnap` replaces `unitSnap` and `gridSnap` for it in `snapUnitIn`; `incOfIn`, `lighterLoadIn` and `heavierLoadIn` step on it; `moveIn` is the shared tail of a moved suggestion (load, a reps target turned into a load target when heavier, the step named in the words).
- **Breaks in two ranges:** `rangeInUse(full, want)` measures the 42-day window to the last session before a break; `suggestCoreIn` and `backDaysIn` read it.
- **Undo:** `ACT.undo` merges the draft from the three versions normalized.
- **Demo:** `ACT.demoLoad` does nothing but say why while `acct.user` is set.

### r56 (2026-10-21, version 2.8; phase 4, PENDING 331)

- **The log as every build reads it:** `normalize`, `keepExtra`, `cleanCardio`, `cleanSess`, `freshState`, `starterRoutine`, `starterSigs`, `tplSigs` and `plainTemplate` in `src/app/normalize.ts`; `normalize.dropped` and `keepExtra.n` are properties of the functions, as before. Raw values are read through `rec`, `arr` and `parseJson` (`records.ts`) and `spread` (`normalize.ts`); `oneOf`, `isMuscle` and `isMuscleOrJoint` are the membership tests, a Set of the keys behind them.
- **The save on this device:** `src/app/storage.ts`: `ENV`, `KEY`, `LOGK`, `WKVIEW_KEY`, `DEMOSET_KEY`; `device` (`ok`, `boot`, `hadLocal`, `hadLog`, `lastSaved`: the flags `storageOk`, `storageBoot`, `bootHadLocal`, `HAD_LOG` and `lastSaved` were); `logBuf`, `logCut`, `log`; `storageTest`, `load`; `undoStack`, `snapshot`, `capturePost`, `undoPersist`, `undoRestore`, `undoForget`; `demoFirstRun`, `demoKeep`, `demoRestore`; `backupDue`, `saveStateNow`; `storageStart` (what the storage part ran at load); `clearTimer` (clearTimeout of a timer that may be null).
- **Saving and the account's copy:** `src/app/sync.ts`: `saveNow`, `saveSoon`, `commit`; `SYNC_KEY`, `saveMeta`, `notesIn` (the notes as saved, the casts until 305); `assemble`, `cq`, `withTimeout`, `setCloud`, `baseOf`, `mergeChunk`, `trackEdits`, `resyncLocalH`, `conflictNote`, `hasUnsent`, `cloudSoon`, `cloudFlush`, `cloudPull`, `cloudChecked`, `cloudInit`, `cloudStop`; `syncStart` (the notes read, the device id, this device's hashes, the listeners `onStorage`, `onVisibility`, `onPageHide`). Since r60's review every write of the log goes through `writeLocal`, which notes it as the last saved (`device.lastSaved`, the base another tab's save is merged against); `saveNow` first merges a save another tab made and this one has not yet heard (`storedElsewhere`, then `mergeIn` once the change in hand has run), and `onStorage` passes by a write since replaced: until then a tab saving before it heard the other's replaced it, and two tabs on one workout each lost one of a typed load and a tick (`tests/r60.js` section 8, on r59 too for an untick).
- **The screens, from the modules:** `fx` and `setFx` in `store.ts` (`render`, `rerenderKeepScroll`, `toast`, `setSaveState`, `syncLine`, `obGate`), filled by the first statement of the legacy script (`src/10-start.js`), then `storageStart()`, `syncStart()`. `syncLine` (`src/14-account.js`) is the sync line redrawn in place; `setSaveState` is in `src/18-ui.js`.
- **Types:** `Settings`, `Measurement` (`types.ts`); `State` gained `version`, `settings: Settings`, `measurements`, `draft: DraftLike | null`; `Cloud` typed field by field, with `CloudDb`, `CloudRef`, `CloudSnap`, `SyncNote`, `Base` (`store.ts`); the page's services declared in `providers.ts` (`ironlogAuth` with `signOut`, `claude.use`, `ironlogCloud`), which is now layer 3.
- **Helpers that read documents:** `merge3List` and `unionById` take their lists as they come (a list field that is no list reads as empty); `emptyChunk` returns a `Rec`; `bodyChunkOf` takes any value; `did` takes no position; `Dated.t` may be absent.
- **Checks:** `tests/r56.js` (the moved code against `baselines/r55.html`); `tests/hygiene.js` rule 3 counts a logged catch as handled. `tests/r43.js`'s simulated next build changes `src/app/normalize.ts` now (the exercise record moved there): `scripts/join.js`'s `build({ override })` takes a module (`app/<name>.ts`) as well as a part, writing it into a copy of `src/` the bundle reads, since the synchronous bundle takes no plugin.

### r56.1 (2026-10-21, version 2.9; the sync changes after the move, PENDING 332)

- **One workout on two phones:** the draft's sync note keeps the copy both sides agreed on (`Base.draft`, a copy of its own through a JSON round trip in `baseOf`: normalize rebuilds a workout in place and the logger edits it in place, and the first build held the live workout in the note, so the base moved with every tick); `mergeChunk('draft')` merges the same workout exercise by exercise and row by row (`mergeWorkout`, `mergeExercises`, `mergeRows`, `mergeFields` in `merge.ts`; rows keyed by `rid`, set by `newRow` in `session.ts`, kept last on draft and saved sets so a build before r56.1 keeps it as an unknown field); a workout closed on one side stays closed; `keepLost` (sync.ts) keeps a losing copy's ticked sets, joined to the session of its id (`mergeSession`; `sessionFromCloud` reads its half-month document first in a flush) or saved as a session of their own, and notes the edit (`edited`); `draftNote` and `lostNote` say what became of the sets.
- **The account question:** `cloudInit` asks through `fx.ask` (`store.ts`; `confirmBox` and `closeModal` in `src/18-ui.js` carry `onClose`, `openModal` and `crashRetry` read an unanswered question as Cancel); the log kept aside per account: `keepAside`, `takeAside`, `asideList`, `asideClear` and `ASIDE_KEY` in `storage.ts`; `holdsLog` and `joinLogs` in `sync.ts`; `asideLine` in `src/app/yourdata.ts` (layer 4), written into Your data; `resetAll` clears the logs kept aside.
- **Typed checks (305):** `stringsIn`, `baseIn`, `noteIn`, `notesIn(m, from)` in `sync.ts`; `logEntryIn` in `storage.ts`; `CARDIO_KINDS`, `CARDIO_WHENS`, `isTime` in `normalize.ts`; `tests/hygiene.js` refuses `localStorage`, `indexedDB` and `JSON.parse` outside `storage.ts`, `sync.ts` and `records.ts`.
- **Types:** `CloudStatus` (307), `LogEntry`, `DraftSet.rid`, `LoggedSet.rid`, `Base.draft`, `Fx.ask`; `Dated.t` and `startedAt` through `isTime` (308).
- **Checks:** `tests/r56-1.js` (two phones on a mock cloud; fails on `baselines/r56.html` at its first check), `tests/r56-1b.js` (the question through the real account service, against the stand-in server); `tests/r56.js` pinned to r55 against `baselines/r56.html`.

### r60 (2026-10-25, version 2.14; phase 5's last screen, PENDING 347)

- **The session screen:** `viewWorkout` (was `viewLogger`) and its parts, the taps (`WORKOUT_ACTS`, the menu's own moves and removal among them) and the fields (`workoutInput`, `workoutChange`, `workoutKey`; the focus, blur and touch listeners added by `workoutStart`) in `workout.ts`; a set and what a tick changes in `worksets.ts` (`setDone`, the one place a set is ticked, with `noLoadYet` (344), `settleLoad`, `assessSet` applying `nextSets`, `rowPR`, `livePR`, `blockProgress`, `greyR`, `lastRFor`, `newBlock`, `refreshBlock`, `refreshEx`, `syncDraftPlan`, `unitWords`, `orderAt`, `orderRun`, `moveBlock`, `acceptGrey`); Finish in `workfinish.ts` (`finish`, `finishNow`, `saveSession`, the recap); the drag in `workdrag.ts` (`mountBlockDrag`, `blockDragStop`); what they change between draws in `workstate.ts` (`W`: the auto-mark waiting, the marked load field, the effort strip, the plates line, the folds; the block handles `blkRef`, `blkIdx`; `redraw`, the hook the screen gives at start). The legacy in-place patches (`afterSet`, `updateSessBar`, `refreshPR`, `refreshGrey` and the like) went: the screen is drawn again and lit-html changes what differs.
- **The next sets after a set:** `nextSetsIn` in `engine.ts` (from `src/17-adjust.js`, deleted), with `stretchTopIn` (341 b, rule 3) and the first session back's aim (rule 4); the session after a return, `sgAfterReturnIn` (rule 5), first among `suggestCoreIn`'s cases; `bwNoLoadIn`.
- **Fields:** `fieldValue` keeps text typed and not yet confirmed (`viewStart` notes the page's input, change and focusout, view.ts) and text the screen put there to be typed over (`fieldShown`, the grey load selected on a tap, 341 a). A load field focused is selected (`workoutFocus`), the tap's release kept from moving the caret (`workoutUp`), and its first key made over the whole number by the browser's own editing (`workoutBeforeInput`, `W.replaceNext`), so the key replaces it whatever the selection shows and the field's change still follows: GitHub's WebKit run of r60's first push typed 1135 over a selected 135 and 1152.535 over a grey 135.
- **The write path:** `act` runs a change made inside another as part of it (one Undo step, one save at the end; found by r60's full run in `tests/r44.js`).
- **Elsewhere:** the update bar in `update.ts` (`updateStart`, `UPDATE_ACTS`; "Update ready" with Update); normalize's draft blocks built against `DraftBlock` (`satisfies`), `cleanTgt` returning a `Target`; the installed build's library loaders list its own copies alone (`scripts/build.js`).
- **Checks:** `tests/r60.js` (section 1 holds the screen to r59 in 35 sessions and modes; sections 2 to 7 fail on r59 or on a build before r60), `tests/inflight.js`, `tests/personas.js`, `tests/loadrules.js`; `tests/engine.js` calls `nextSets` and `bwNoLoad` in Node, with two return histories.

### r72 (2026-11-04, version 2.27; V's report of the fold's jump on live r71 and the 387 audit of the engines, with V's rulings of 2026-11-04; tracker rows NEW-63, 387-B1 to 387-B7, 387-B9, 387-C1, 387-D)

- **The fold's glide (NEW-63):** `autoFold` (worksets.ts) hands the next exercise to `foldGlide`, which holds the space the fold took away as a margin above it and gives it back over `FOLD_MS.glide` while the page scrolls, ending where `scrollIntoView` put it (the exercise's scroll margin and the page's scroll padding); a touch or a wheel stops it, reduced motion goes straight there, a focused field in it keeps it still. Until r71 the anchoring `scrollBy` stopped at the top when the fold took away more than the page was scrolled, so the screen showed the session's title and glided back down (r60 and later).
- **Loads in the other unit (387-B1):** `unitGridIn` snaps only on a step set in the unit on screen (`onStep` of the step in that unit, `TYPED_STEP`: the unit's own, or 10 lb on a lb plan, r64's R-2); a step set in the other unit keeps its loads from the last one, shown converted.
- **The engine's cases (387-B2, B5, B6):** `sgBelowRangeIn` reads `fitE`; the target's `tp` is the sets as done (the grey reps keep `repFloor` in analytics.ts), and sgRepsIn's "(a set under N counts as N)" is gone; `suggestTimedIn` has `noAdd`, with words for building time and a harder hold past `REPS_MAXED`.
- **Readings (387-B3, B4, C1):** `readingOf` and `repsOf` read `triedAt`, a missed set as 0, so its session reads below its usual and the target repeats, as after a near miss (V: "do whatever is not slapping on confusing workaround rules"; CLAUDE.md's missed-set line restated); `prScoreOf` in formulas.ts is the one PR score (`prScore` in analytics.ts, `rowPR` in worksets.ts; `epley` is no longer exported); `idxPRsIn`'s fallback when no set counts a PR is not counted (found building B4: judged as a whole, a best estimate was counted by another set's strength, `tests/r64.js` section 15); `idxAutoLighter`'s new-level run is counted within a rep range (`removeWhere`).
- **normalize (387-B7):** library records a save lacks are appended through `cleanExercise`.
- **Documents (387-B9, 387-D):** SLOW_RECOVERY's citation dropped; EVIDENCE.md's Currier, Nuzzo and Pelland wording corrected, r72's conventions added, `prScoreOf`, `WARM_SHARE` and `TIMED_MAX_MIN` listed; the Guide's missed set, rep floor, new level and bodyweight hold lines.
- **From the independent review (R72-1 to R72-8):** the new-level run is the lighter sessions read against any of the same earlier sessions (`readAgainst`, R72-1: an imported history, no range, is the usual of every range); rowPR hides a PR only behind an earlier PR of the session (R72-2); `prScoreOf` uses `e1At` with the reserve read up to 4 (R72-3); `foldGlide`'s stop goes to the glide's end where the page cannot keep the next exercise in place (R72-5); `noAdd` is read once from the whole log and passed to `suggestTimedIn` (R72-6); cleanExercise drops the cleaned main muscle from the helpers (R72-7); `readingOf` reads 0 when every set read was missed (R72-8). R72-4 (V, 2026-11-04, "Count heavier as PR"): `prScoreOf` reads no further than `E1_MAX_REPS` to failure, and prJudge's big and rowPR count a tie by its reps at its load (`prDominated`). Then, shown that a rep record at a lighter load (100 x 18 after 120 x 8) is no longer counted, V: "Strict: not counted"; `tests/r34b.js` 1g (80 x 10 after 75 x 15 now a Weight PR) and `tests/r53.js` (100 x 18 no counted PR) held to it. `tests/r72.js` sections 10 to 16.
- **The missed set's words (NEW-66):** `MISSED_SAID` and the Guide say a missed set never earns a step up; "keeps the load from going up" read false once 387-B3 let a step earned before stand.
- **Checks:** `tests/r72.js` sections 1 to 9, each failing on r71; persona 16 in `tests/personas.js` (fails on r71 in 5 checks) and coherence's One PR, every screen (fails on r71 on the edge log), as rule 3 asks. Held to V's rulings (each assertion's old value named in it): `tests/r64a.js` R-1 (the target repeats, 195, as after a near miss; a missed set alone never steps up), `tests/r32.js` 195 and W6 (the reps done, 16+ and 24) and W4 (90 x 12 to failure is no PR; at 3 in reserve it is), `tests/r54b.js` (18+), `tests/r61.js` sections 2 (the Guide's new level) and 4 (library lifts appended with inc and plates).

### r66 (2026-11-01, version 2.21; PENDING 372 to 378 and 368, with NEW-30, NEW-35 and NEW-36)

- **Bodyweight, asked for where it matters (372, V: "We should not require it, but it should be more obvious"):** `src/app/bwnudge.ts`. Today's bodyweight card from the first day a bodyweight lift is in the routine in use (`bwDueCard`, today.ts), a line on each bodyweight lift in a session until a weigh-in exists (`bwWarn`, its Set it opening the `bw` sheet), a note once a visit when one is added (a toast, never a sheet: a sheet blocked persona 4's taps; since 2.47 `bwNote`, a sentence in the message about what was added to a plan, never a toast over another message, and none after a routine is picked or in a session, where a card or line asks: NEW-116), and a field in Settings (`bwSettings`). A weigh-in saved anywhere is today's weigh-in (`addBW`).
- **Pictures on notes, phone only (373, V's pick):** ids in the log (`pics`, last in a session and in each exercise, absent when none: `picsField`, `KNOWN_SESS`, `KNOWN_SEX`, `KNOWN_BLOCK`, so `keepExtra` never brings back one taken off), bytes in IndexedDB (`${STORAGE_ID}-pics`, `picRowsRead` and `picRowsWrite` in storage.ts, the rest in `src/app/pics.ts`). Every picture is decoded and drawn again as a JPEG at 1600 px at most (location and anything hidden dropped), SVG and non-photos refused. PIC_MAX (4) is the room + Picture gives a note; a record keeps up to 40 ids, since a lift done twice in a row is saved as one with both notes' pictures (R66-2); no id is ever taken off to make room (R66-3). A picture belongs to the log it was last named by (`own`: `cloud.acct` or ''), and only that log's tidy drops it, 45 days after it was last named (R66-11). Another tab's pictures are read when its change arrives or the page returns (`picsRefresh`, events.ts). Undo puts the workout's pictures back (`mergeDraft`, R66-1). The strip and the large view are `src/app/notepics.ts`; History shows them read-only.
- **The Stitch elements (2.33, V 2026-11-07, PENDING.md's ST rows):** V picked parts of Google Stitch drafts to bring onto the app's own screens, layouts and flow unchanged. The profile photo is `src/app/photo.ts`: one 256 px square JPEG under `KEY + '.photo'` (`PHOTO_KEY`), through the same redraw as a note's picture (`jpegOf` in pics.ts), read back only when it is a JPEG data address, shown in the menu button (`setSaveState`, page.ts) and in the menu's header, beside its title, with Remove photo (`photoHead`, sheetsaccount.ts; a row of its own made the menu too tall for a 320 x 568 phone, tests/r54b.js), never in the log; Erase everything removes it. Today's one-time line is `photoBar` (today.ts), signed in only, behind the install and new-icon lines, answered for good by Not now or a photo (`PHOTO_ASKED`). The progress gradient is `--holo` and `--holo-1` to `--holo-4` in the style sheet: `#holoG` in the page's markup for the week ring and the rest dial (laid over a circle's box), the bars' `--pct` sizing it to the track, and `holoLine` (view.ts) for line charts, drawn inside each chart across its width because a gradient laid over a flat line's box (no height) is not drawn. PRs are gold (`.tag.gold`, the set row's bar, History's `hpr`); History's count per session is the counted PRs (`p.big`), the same list Today and Stats count (tests/coherence.js 1b). "Rep hunt" and "Hold weight" were not added: the engine has no such states (its tags are Load due, Stalled, At max load and PR last time).
- **2.34 (build r80):** the profile photo is the account's (R79-1, V 2026-11-08: "we should be treating it like an account"): `photo.ts` keeps the phone's copy (`PHOTO_KEY`) and a note of where it stands against the account (`PHOTO_SYNC`: the account, when it changed, whether a change is to be sent); `sync.ts` (`photoReconcile`, `photoSend`, `photoSyncSoon`) settles it with the account's own `photo` document (`PHOTO_DOC`, chunks.ts, never a log id) on every pull and after a change here, the later change kept, a write landing only over the copy read (setIf); sign out and Delete my account take it off the phone (`photoForget`), Erase everything while syncing removes it from the account, and the menu offers it only signed in (tests/r80b.js). Your gym takes any plate (NEW-76): `gymClean` keeps any weight in whole hundredths up to 50 kg or 100 lb (`plateOk`), and `plateGridIn` gives every load the plates make, any number of each, up to 250 kg or 550 lb a side, as a list the engine's plates kind reads (`loadsForIn`) by the rules the arithmetic grid was read by, so on plates that are whole multiples of the smallest every answer is 2.33's (tests/r80.js section 2a, 4,566 cases); the plates line keeps greedy's order and finds the combination greedy misses (`platesPerSide`). Short on time says when nothing can come off and why (R79-2). ### r65.2 (2026-10-31, version 2.20; PENDING 369 and 370)

- **An app, not a web page (369):** the root sets `touch-action: manipulation`, so a double tap is a tap and never a zoom (pinch stays: iOS keeps it for accessibility); the page selects no text and opens no Copy and Look Up menu on a long press, and a tap leaves no grey flash; fields, text boxes and anything editable stay selectable (src/02-style.css, after the root's rules). The page's bounce at its edges is kept, as native iPhone apps have it. `tests/r65-2.js` sections 1 to 3.
- **Three more accents (370, V: "Only add in the new colors now. Leave out gold"):** Violet (white text, 4.8:1), Cyan and Red (dark text, white on them is under 4.5:1). With Cyan the blue status colour (above target, information, supersets) turns violet, as with Blue; with Red the red one (errors, warnings, Delete and Discard, a planned day missed on the week bar and the calendar, a falling trend) turns magenta (V's pick), so the accent never reads as a status. A set ticked with 0 reps keeps the accent's tick, as in every accent, its word "missed" saying so (I first wrote "missed sets" turn magenta, to V too; corrected the same day, NEW-26). Picked from the look picker, where V rejected every other change shown (steel card, electric looks) and Gold (close to under target's amber). The accent list is in `ACCENTS` (layout.ts), the normalize pick, the Settings type and Settings' action; `tests/r65-2.js` section 4, and the accent loops of `tests/design.js`, `tests/r28.js`, `tests/r29b.js` and `tests/essentials.js`.
